   54 | function hasBrowserWindow(): boolean {
   55 |   return typeof globalThis.window !== "undefined";
   56 | }
   57 | 
   58 | function getBrowserWindow(): Window | null {
   59 |   return hasBrowserWindow() ? globalThis.window : null;
   60 | }
   61 | 
   62 | function prefersReducedMotion(): boolean {
   63 |   const browserWindow = getBrowserWindow();
   64 |   if (!browserWindow) return true;
   65 | 
   66 |   return (
   67 |     browserWindow.matchMedia?.("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)")?.matches ??
   68 |     false
   69 |   );
   70 | }
   71 | 
   72 | function useMediaQuery(query: string): boolean {
   73 |   const get = () => getBrowserWindow()?.matchMedia(query).matches ?? false;
   74 | 
   75 |   const [matches, setMatches] = React.useState<boolean>(get);
   76 | 
   77 |   React.useEffect(() => {
   78 |     const browserWindow = getBrowserWindow();
   79 |     if (!browserWindow) return;
   80 | 
   81 |     const mediaQueryList = browserWindow.matchMedia(query);
   82 |     const onChange = () => setMatches(mediaQueryList.matches);
   83 | 
   84 |     setMatches(mediaQueryList.matches);
   85 |     mediaQueryList.addEventListener("change", onChange);
   86 | 
   87 |     return () => mediaQueryList.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
   88 |   }, [query]);
   89 | 
   90 |   return matches;
   91 | }

  510 |     if (c.createdByMemberId !== viewerMemberId) return;
  511 |     if (c.status !== "live") return;

  513 |     setEditMountKey((n) => n + 1);
  514 |     setEditByCommentId((prev) => {
  515 |       const cur = prev[c.id];
  516 |       const base: EditDraft = cur ?? {
  517 |         open: true,
  518 |         ui: "basic",
  519 |         plain: c.bodyPlain ?? "",
  520 |         doc: isTipTapDoc(c.bodyRich) ? c.bodyRich : null,
  521 |         posting: false,
  522 |         err: "",
  523 |       };
  524 |       return {
  525 |         ...prev,
  526 |         [c.id]: {
  527 |           ...base,
  528 |           open: true,
  529 |           plain: c.bodyPlain ?? base.plain,
  530 |           doc: isTipTapDoc(c.bodyRich) ? c.bodyRich : base.doc,
  531 |           err: "",
  532 |         },
  533 |       };
  534 |     });
  535 |   }

  537 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  538 |     function targetNodeFromEvent(e: MouseEvent): Node | null {
  539 |       return e.target instanceof Node ? e.target : null;
  540 |     }
  541 | 
  542 |     function closeComposerIfEmpty(target: Node) {
  543 |       const composerEl = composerWrapRef.current;
  544 |       const clickedInComposer = Boolean(composerEl?.contains(target));
  545 |       const hasDraft = Boolean(draftRef.current.trim());
  546 | 
  547 |       if (!clickedInComposer && !hasDraft) {
  548 |         setComposerStage("collapsed");
  549 |       }
  550 |     }
  551 | 
  552 |     function closeEmptyReplies(target: Node) {
  553 |       for (const [commentId, draftState] of Object.entries(
  554 |         replyDraftsRef.current,
  555 |       )) {
  556 |         const wrapper = replyWrapByIdRef.current[commentId];
  557 |         const shouldClose =
  558 |           Boolean(draftState?.open) &&
  559 |           Boolean(wrapper) &&
  560 |           !wrapper?.contains(target) &&
  561 |           !draftState.plain.trim();
  562 | 
  563 |         if (shouldClose) deleteReplyDraft(commentId);
  564 |       }
  565 |     }
  566 | 
  567 |     function closeEmptyEdits(target: Node) {
  568 |       for (const [commentId, draftState] of Object.entries(
  569 |         editDraftsRef.current,
  570 |       )) {
  571 |         const wrapper = editWrapByIdRef.current[commentId];
  572 |         const shouldClose =
  573 |           Boolean(draftState?.open) &&
  574 |           Boolean(wrapper) &&
  575 |           !wrapper?.contains(target) &&
  576 |           !draftState.plain.trim();
  577 | 
  578 |         if (shouldClose) deleteEditDraft(commentId);
  579 |       }
  580 |     }
  581 | 
  582 |     function closeAutoClosableReports(target: Node) {
  583 |       for (const [commentId, draftState] of Object.entries(
  584 |         reportDraftsRef.current,
  585 |       )) {
  586 |         const wrapper = reportWrapByIdRef.current[commentId];
  587 |         const canAutoClose =
  588 |           Boolean(draftState.done) || !draftState.reason.trim();
  589 |         const shouldClose =
  590 |           draftState.open &&
  591 |           Boolean(wrapper) &&
  592 |           !wrapper?.contains(target) &&
  593 |           canAutoClose;
  594 | 
  595 |         if (shouldClose) deleteReportDraft(commentId);
  596 |       }
  597 |     }
  598 | 
  599 |     function onMouseDown(e: MouseEvent) {
  600 |       const target = targetNodeFromEvent(e);
  601 |       if (!target) return;
  602 | 
  603 |       closeComposerIfEmpty(target);
  604 |       closeEmptyReplies(target);
  605 |       closeEmptyEdits(target);
  606 |       closeAutoClosableReports(target);
  607 |     }

  609 |     globalThis.document.addEventListener("mousedown", onMouseDown, true);
  610 |     return () =>
  611 |       globalThis.document.removeEventListener("mousedown", onMouseDown, true);
  612 |   }, []);

  634 |       if (!el) continue;

    1 | "use client";
    2 | 
    3 | import React from "react";

    7 | export type ExegesisSelectedLine = {

   14 | const EXEGESIS_HASH_NAV_EVENT = "af:exegesis-hash-navigation";
   15 | const EXEGESIS_PENDING_LINE_KEY = "af:exegesis-pending-line";
   16 | 
   17 | type ExegesisHashNavigationDetail = Readonly<{
   18 |   lineKey?: string;
   19 |   commentId?: string;
   20 |   rootId?: string;
   21 | }>;
   22 | 
   23 | function readPendingLineKey(): string {
   24 |   const raw = globalThis.window.sessionStorage.getItem(
   25 |     EXEGESIS_PENDING_LINE_KEY,
   26 |   );
   27 | 
   28 |   if (!raw) return "";
   29 | 
   30 |   try {
   31 |     const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(raw);
   32 |     if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== "object") return "";
   33 | 
   34 |     const record = parsed as Record<string, unknown>;
   35 |     return typeof record.lineKey === "string" ? record.lineKey.trim() : "";
   36 |   } catch {
   37 |     return "";
   38 |   }
   39 | }
   40 | 
   41 | function clearPendingLineKey() {
   42 |   globalThis.window.sessionStorage.removeItem(EXEGESIS_PENDING_LINE_KEY);
   43 | }
   44 | 
   45 | function isHashNavigationDetail(
   46 |   value: unknown,
   47 | ): value is ExegesisHashNavigationDetail {
   48 |   if (!value || typeof value !== "object") return false;
   49 | 
   50 |   const record = value as Record<string, unknown>;
   51 | 
   52 |   return (
   53 |     (record.lineKey == null || typeof record.lineKey === "string") &&
   54 |     (record.commentId == null || typeof record.commentId === "string") &&
   55 |     (record.rootId == null || typeof record.rootId === "string")
   56 |   );
   57 | }
   58 | 
   59 | export default function useExegesisHashState(props: {
   60 |   lyrics: LyricsApiOk;
   61 |   setSelected: React.Dispatch<
   62 |     React.SetStateAction<ExegesisSelectedLine | null>
   63 |   >;
   64 |   setFocusedRootId: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<string>>;
   65 |   isMobile: boolean;
   66 |   selectedLineKey: string;
   67 |   setDrawerOpen: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<boolean>>;
   68 | }) {
   69 |   const {
   70 |     lyrics,
   71 |     setSelected,
   72 |     setFocusedRootId,
   73 |     isMobile,
   74 |     selectedLineKey,
   75 |     setDrawerOpen,
   76 |   } = props;
   77 | 
   78 |   const pendingScrollCommentIdRef = React.useRef<string>("");
   79 |   const openFromHashRef = React.useRef<boolean>(false);
   80 | 
   81 |   const { recordingId: lyricsRecordingId, cues, groupMap } = lyrics;
   82 | 
   83 |   React.useEffect(() => {
   84 |     if (!cues || cues.length === 0) return;
   85 | 
   86 |     function applyHashState(detail?: ExegesisHashNavigationDetail) {
   87 |       const h = parseHash();
   88 |       const pendingLineKey = readPendingLineKey();
   89 | 
   90 |       const lineKey = (
   91 |         detail?.lineKey ??
   92 |         pendingLineKey ??
   93 |         h.lineKey ??
   94 |         ""
   95 |       ).trim();
   96 |       const commentId = (detail?.commentId ?? h.commentId ?? "").trim();
   97 |       const rootId = (detail?.rootId ?? h.rootId ?? "").trim();
   98 | 
   99 |       setFocusedRootId(rootId);
  100 | 
  101 |       if (!lineKey) {
  102 |         setSelected(null);
  103 |         return;
  104 |       }
  105 | 
  106 |       const pick = cues.find((c) => c.lineKey === lineKey);
  107 |       if (!pick) {
  108 |         setSelected(null);
  109 |         return;
  110 |       }
  111 | 
  112 |       if (pendingLineKey === lineKey) {
  113 |         clearPendingLineKey();
  114 |       }
  115 | 
  116 |       setSelected({
  117 |         lineKey: pick.lineKey,
  118 |         lineText: pick.text,
  119 |         tMs: pick.tMs,
  120 |         groupKey:
  121 |           (groupMap?.[pick.lineKey]?.canonicalGroupKey ??
  122 |             pick.canonicalGroupKey ??
  123 |             "") ||
  124 |           undefined,
  125 |       });
  126 | 
  127 |       pendingScrollCommentIdRef.current = commentId;
  128 |       openFromHashRef.current = true;
  129 |     }
  130 | 
  131 |     function onNativeHashNavigation() {
  132 |       applyHashState();
  133 |     }
  134 | 
  135 |     function onAppHashNavigation(event: Event) {
  136 |       const detail =
  137 |         event instanceof CustomEvent && isHashNavigationDetail(event.detail)
  138 |           ? event.detail
  139 |           : undefined;
  140 | 
  141 |       applyHashState(detail);
  142 |     }
  143 | 
  144 |     applyHashState();
  145 | 
  146 |     globalThis.window.addEventListener("hashchange", onNativeHashNavigation);
  147 |     globalThis.window.addEventListener("popstate", onNativeHashNavigation);
  148 |     globalThis.window.addEventListener(
  149 |       EXEGESIS_HASH_NAV_EVENT,
  150 |       onAppHashNavigation,
  151 |     );
  152 | 
  153 |     return () => {
  154 |       globalThis.window.removeEventListener(
  155 |         "hashchange",
  156 |         onNativeHashNavigation,
  157 |       );
  158 |       globalThis.window.removeEventListener("popstate", onNativeHashNavigation);
  159 |       globalThis.window.removeEventListener(
  160 |         EXEGESIS_HASH_NAV_EVENT,
  161 |         onAppHashNavigation,
  162 |       );
  163 |     };
  164 |   }, [lyricsRecordingId, cues, groupMap, setSelected, setFocusedRootId]);
  165 | 
  166 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  167 |     if (!isMobile) return;
  168 |     if (!selectedLineKey) return;
  169 |     if (!openFromHashRef.current) return;
  170 | 
  171 |     setDrawerOpen(true);
  172 |     openFromHashRef.current = false;
  173 |   }, [isMobile, selectedLineKey, setDrawerOpen]);
  174 | 
  175 |   return {
  176 |     pendingScrollCommentIdRef,
  177 |   };
  178 | }

   53 | function isApiErrShape(v: unknown): v is ApiErrShape {
   54 |   if (typeof v !== "object" || v === null) return false;
   55 |   const r = v as Record<string, unknown>;
   56 |   return r.ok === false && typeof r.error === "string";
   57 | }
   58 | 
   59 | function assertOk<T extends { ok: true }>(
   60 |   j: unknown,
   61 |   fallbackMsg: string,
   62 | ): asserts j is T {
   63 |   if (typeof j !== "object" || j === null) throw new Error(fallbackMsg);
   64 |   const r = j as Record<string, unknown>;
   65 |   if (r.ok !== true) {
   66 |     if (isApiErrShape(j)) throw new Error(j.error || fallbackMsg);
   67 |     throw new Error(fallbackMsg);
   68 |   }

   99 | function fmtTimeMs(ms: number): string {
  100 |   if (!Number.isFinite(ms)) return String(ms);
  101 |   const s = Math.max(0, Math.floor(ms / 1000));
  102 |   const m = Math.floor(s / 60);
  103 |   const r = s % 60;
  104 |   return `${m}:${String(r).padStart(2, "0")}`;
  105 | }

  107 | function normalize(s: string): string {
  108 |   return (s ?? "").toLowerCase().trim();
  109 | }
  110 | 
  111 | function startsWithLk(groupKey: string): boolean {
  112 |   return normalize(groupKey).startsWith("lk:");
  113 | }
  114 | 
  115 | function isProbablyCanonicalGroupKey(groupKey: string): boolean {
  116 |   const g = normalize(groupKey);
  117 |   if (!g) return false;
  118 |   if (startsWithLk(g)) return false;
  119 |   // Your new keys look like g:..., but don’t hard-require it.
  120 |   // Prefer server-reported canonical groups as truth, and use this only for display.
  121 |   return g.startsWith("g:") || g.startsWith("cg:") || g.startsWith("grp:");
  122 | }

  280 |     const out: Record<string, string> = {};
  281 |     const m = groupMap?.map ?? {};
  282 |     for (const lk of Object.keys(m)) {
  283 |       const gk = m[lk]?.canonicalGroupKey;
  284 |       if (typeof gk === "string" && gk.trim()) out[lk] = gk;
  285 |     }
  286 |     return out;
  287 |   }, [groupMap?.map]);
  288 | 
  289 |   const getLineGroupKey = React.useCallback(
  290 |     (lineKey: string): string => {
  291 |       const mapped = lineToGroupKey[lineKey];
  292 |       if (mapped) return mapped;
  293 |       return `lk:${lineKey}`;
  294 |     },
  295 |     [lineToGroupKey],
  296 |   );
  297 | 
  298 |   const stats = React.useMemo(() => {
  299 |     let mappedCount = 0;
  300 |     for (const c of cues) {
  301 |       if (Boolean(lineToGroupKey[c.lineKey])) mappedCount += 1;
  302 |     }
  303 |     const total = cues.length;
  304 |     const unmapped = Math.max(0, total - mappedCount);
  305 |     const canonCount = (groupMap?.groups ?? []).length;
  306 |     return { total, mappedCount, unmapped, canonCount };
  307 |   }, [cues, lineToGroupKey, groupMap?.groups]);
  308 | 
  309 |   const derivedGroups: DerivedGroup[] = React.useMemo(() => {
  310 |     const members: Record<string, string[]> = {};
  311 |     for (const c of cues) {
  312 |       const gk = getLineGroupKey(c.lineKey);
  313 |       if (!members[gk]) members[gk] = [];
  314 |       members[gk].push(c.lineKey);
  315 |     }
  316 | 
  317 |     const out: DerivedGroup[] = [];
  318 |     const keys = Object.keys(members);
  319 | 
  320 |     for (const key of keys) {
  321 |       const meta = canonicalMetaByKey[key];
  322 |       const isCanonical = Boolean(meta) || isProbablyCanonicalGroupKey(key);
  323 |       out.push({
  324 |         key,
  325 |         isCanonical: Boolean(meta) || (isCanonical && !startsWithLk(key)),
  326 |         count: members[key]?.length ?? 0,
  327 |         updatedAt: meta?.updatedAt ?? null,
  328 |         lineKeys: members[key] ?? [],
  329 |       });
  330 |     }
  331 | 
  332 |     // Sort: canonical first, then by count desc, then by updatedAt desc, then key
  333 |     out.sort((a, b) => {
  334 |       if (a.isCanonical !== b.isCanonical) return a.isCanonical ? -1 : 1;
  335 |       if (a.count !== b.count) return b.count - a.count;
  336 |       const ta = a.updatedAt ? Date.parse(a.updatedAt) : 0;
  337 |       const tb = b.updatedAt ? Date.parse(b.updatedAt) : 0;
  338 |       if (ta !== tb) return tb - ta;
  339 |       return a.key.localeCompare(b.key);
  340 |     });
  341 | 
  342 |     // Filter search
  343 |     const q = normalize(groupSearch);
  344 |     if (!q) return out;
  345 |     return out.filter((g) => normalize(g.key).includes(q));
  346 |   }, [cues, canonicalMetaByKey, groupSearch, getLineGroupKey]);
  347 | 
  348 |   const activeGroup: DerivedGroup | null = React.useMemo(() => {
  349 |     const gk = activeGroupKey.trim();
  350 |     if (!gk) return null;
  351 |     return derivedGroups.find((g) => g.key === gk) ?? null;
  352 |   }, [activeGroupKey, derivedGroups]);
  353 | 
  354 |   const cueByLineKey: Record<string, LyricsApiCue> = React.useMemo(() => {
  355 |     const out: Record<string, LyricsApiCue> = {};
  356 |     for (const c of cues) out[c.lineKey] = c;
  357 |     return out;
  358 |   }, [cues]);
  359 | 
  360 |   const visibleCues: Array<{ cue: LyricsApiCue; groupKey: string }> =
  361 |     React.useMemo(() => {
  362 |       const onlyUnmapped = showOnlyUnmapped;
  363 |       const onlySelected = showOnlySelected;
  364 | 
  365 |       const out: Array<{ cue: LyricsApiCue; groupKey: string }> = [];
  366 |       for (const c of cues) {
  367 |         const gk = getLineGroupKey(c.lineKey);
  368 |         const isUnmapped = !Boolean(lineToGroupKey[c.lineKey]);
  369 |         const isSel = Boolean(selectedLineKeys[c.lineKey]);
  370 | 
  371 |         if (onlyUnmapped && !isUnmapped) continue;
  372 |         if (onlySelected && !isSel) continue;
  373 | 
  374 |         out.push({ cue: c, groupKey: gk });
  375 |       }
  376 |       return out;
  377 |     }, [
  378 |       cues,
  379 |       getLineGroupKey,
  380 |       lineToGroupKey,
  381 |       selectedLineKeys,
  382 |       showOnlySelected,
  383 |       showOnlyUnmapped,
  384 |     ]);
  385 | 
  386 |   function setSelectionForLineKeys(lineKeys: string[], value: boolean) {
  387 |     setSelectedLineKeys((prev) => {
  388 |       const next: Record<string, boolean> = { ...prev };
  389 |       for (const lk of lineKeys) next[lk] = value;
  390 |       return next;
  391 |     });
  392 |   }
  393 | 
  394 |   function toggleSelect(lineKey: string) {
  395 |     setSelectedLineKeys((prev) => ({
  396 |       ...prev,
  397 |       [lineKey]: !Boolean(prev[lineKey]),
  398 |     }));
  399 |   }
  400 | 
  401 |   function clearSelection() {
  402 |     setSelectedLineKeys({});
  403 |     setLastClickedIdx(null);
  404 |   }
  405 | 
  406 |   function onCueClick(idx: number, lineKey: string, e: React.MouseEvent) {
  407 |     // Shift range select within the *current visible list*
  408 |     if (e.shiftKey && lastClickedIdx !== null) {
  409 |       const lo = Math.min(lastClickedIdx, idx);
  410 |       const hi = Math.max(lastClickedIdx, idx);
  411 |       const slice = visibleCues.slice(lo, hi + 1).map((x) => x.cue.lineKey);
  412 | 
  413 |       const allSelected = slice.every((lk) => Boolean(selectedLineKeys[lk]));
  414 |       setSelectionForLineKeys(slice, !allSelected);
  415 |       setLastClickedIdx(idx);
  416 |       return;
  417 |     }
  418 | 
  419 |     toggleSelect(lineKey);
  420 |     setLastClickedIdx(idx);
  421 |   }
  422 | 
  423 |   async function loadGrouping(seed?: string) {
  424 |     const recordingId = (seed ?? recordingIdInput).trim();
  425 |     if (!recordingId) {
  426 |       setErr("Enter a recordingId for grouping.");
  427 |       return;
  428 |     }
  429 | 
  430 |     setErr("");
  431 |     setBusy(true);
  432 |     try {
  433 |       const lr = await fetch(
  434 |         `/api/lyrics/by-track?recordingId=${encodeURIComponent(recordingId)}`,
  435 |         { cache: "no-store" },
  436 |       );
  437 |       const lj: unknown = await lr.json();
  438 |       assertOk<LyricsApiOk>(lj, "Failed to load lyrics.");
  439 |       setLyrics(lj);
  440 | 
  441 |       const mr = await fetch(
  442 |         `/api/admin/exegesis/group-map?recordingId=${encodeURIComponent(recordingId)}`,
  443 |         { cache: "no-store" },
  444 |       );
  445 |       const mj: unknown = await mr.json();
  446 |       assertOk<GroupMapOk>(mj, "Failed to load group map.");
  447 |       setGroupMap(mj);
  448 | 
  449 |       setLoadedRecordingId(recordingId);
  450 |       setActiveGroupKey("");
  451 |       clearSelection();
  452 |     } catch (e: unknown) {
  453 |       setErr(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Failed to load grouping.");
  454 |       setLyrics(null);
  455 |       setGroupMap(null);
  456 |       setLoadedRecordingId("");
  457 |       setActiveGroupKey("");
  458 |       clearSelection();
  459 |     } finally {
  460 |       setBusy(false);
  461 |     }
  462 |   }
  463 | 
  464 |   async function createNewGroupKey(): Promise<string> {
  465 |     const r = await fetch("/api/admin/exegesis/group-map/new-group", {
  466 |       method: "POST",
  467 |     });
  468 |     const j: unknown = await r.json();
  469 |     if (!isNewGroupOk(j)) {
  470 |       if (isApiErrShape(j))
  471 |         throw new Error(j.error || "Failed to create group.");
  472 |       throw new Error("Failed to create group.");
  473 |     }
  474 |     return j.canonicalGroupKey;
  475 |   }
  476 | 
  477 |   async function assignLineKeysToGroup(
  478 |     recordingId: string,
  479 |     canonicalGroupKey: string,
  480 |     lineKeys: string[],
  481 |   ): Promise<number> {
  482 |     const r = await fetch("/api/admin/exegesis/group-map/set", {
  483 |       method: "POST",
  484 |       headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
  485 |       body: JSON.stringify({ recordingId, canonicalGroupKey, lineKeys }),
  486 |     });
  487 |     const j: unknown = await r.json();
  488 |     if (!isSetOk(j)) {
  489 |       if (isApiErrShape(j)) throw new Error(j.error || "Assign failed.");
  490 |       throw new Error("Assign failed.");
  491 |     }
  492 |     return j.updated;
  493 |   }
  494 | 
  495 |   async function clearLineKeyMappings(recordingId: string, lineKeys: string[]) {
  496 |     const r = await fetch("/api/admin/exegesis/group-map/clear", {
  497 |       method: "POST",
  498 |       headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
  499 |       body: JSON.stringify({ recordingId, lineKeys }),
  500 |     });
  501 |     const j: unknown = await r.json();
  502 |     if (!isClearOk(j)) {
  503 |       if (isApiErrShape(j)) throw new Error(j.error || "Clear failed.");
  504 |       throw new Error("Clear failed.");
  505 |     }
  506 |     return j.deleted;
  507 |   }
  508 | 
  509 |   async function createGroupFromSelection() {
  510 |     const recordingId = recordingIdInput.trim();
  511 |     if (!recordingId) return;
  512 | 
  513 |     if (!lyrics || !groupMap) {
  514 |       setErr("Load a track first.");
  515 |       return;
  516 |     }
  517 |     if (selectedKeys.length === 0) {
  518 |       setErr("Select at least one line.");
  519 |       return;
  520 |     }
  521 | 
  522 |     setErr("");
  523 |     setBusy(true);
  524 |     try {
  525 |       const gk = await createNewGroupKey();
  526 |       await assignLineKeysToGroup(recordingId, gk, selectedKeys);
  527 |       await loadGrouping(recordingId);
  528 |       setActiveGroupKey(gk);
  529 |       // Keep selection so you can immediately confirm what moved
  530 |       setSelectedLineKeys((prev) => ({ ...prev }));
  531 |     } catch (e: unknown) {
  532 |       setErr(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Create group failed.");
  533 |     } finally {
  534 |       setBusy(false);
  535 |     }
  536 |   }
  537 | 
  538 |   async function assignSelectionToActiveGroup() {
  539 |     const recordingId = recordingIdInput.trim();
  540 |     if (!recordingId) return;
  541 | 
  542 |     const gk = activeGroupKey.trim();
  543 |     if (!gk || startsWithLk(gk)) {
  544 |       setErr("Choose a canonical group in the sidebar first.");
  545 |       return;
  546 |     }
  547 |     if (!lyrics || !groupMap) {
  548 |       setErr("Load a track first.");
  549 |       return;
  550 |     }
  551 |     if (selectedKeys.length === 0) {
  552 |       setErr("Select at least one line.");
  553 |       return;
  554 |     }
  555 | 
  556 |     setErr("");
  557 |     setBusy(true);
  558 |     try {
  559 |       await assignLineKeysToGroup(recordingId, gk, selectedKeys);
  560 |       await loadGrouping(recordingId);
  561 |       setActiveGroupKey(gk);
  562 |     } catch (e: unknown) {
  563 |       setErr(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Assign failed.");
  564 |     } finally {
  565 |       setBusy(false);
  566 |     }
  567 |   }
  568 | 
  569 |   async function clearSelectedMapping() {
  570 |     const recordingId = recordingIdInput.trim();
  571 |     if (!recordingId) return;
  572 | 
  573 |     if (!lyrics || !groupMap) {
  574 |       setErr("Load a track first.");
  575 |       return;
  576 |     }
  577 |     if (selectedKeys.length === 0) {
  578 |       setErr("Select at least one line.");
  579 |       return;
  580 |     }
  581 | 
  582 |     setErr("");
  583 |     setBusy(true);
  584 |     try {
  585 |       await clearLineKeyMappings(recordingId, selectedKeys);
  586 |       await loadGrouping(recordingId);
  587 |     } catch (e: unknown) {
  588 |       setErr(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Clear failed.");
  589 |     } finally {
  590 |       setBusy(false);
  591 |     }
  592 |   }
  593 | 
  594 |   async function clearActiveGroupMappings() {
  595 |     const recordingId = recordingIdInput.trim();
  596 |     if (!recordingId) return;
  597 | 
  598 |     if (!lyrics || !groupMap) {
  599 |       setErr("Load a track first.");
  600 |       return;
  601 |     }
  602 |     if (!activeGroup || startsWithLk(activeGroup.key)) {
  603 |       setErr("Choose a canonical group first.");
  604 |       return;
  605 |     }
  606 | 
  607 |     setErr("");
  608 |     setBusy(true);
  609 |     try {
  610 |       await clearLineKeyMappings(recordingId, activeGroup.lineKeys);
  611 |       await loadGrouping(recordingId);
  612 |       setActiveGroupKey("");
  613 |       clearSelection();
  614 |     } catch (e: unknown) {
  615 |       setErr(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Clear group failed.");
  616 |     } finally {
  617 |       setBusy(false);
  618 |     }
  619 |   }
  620 | 
  621 |   async function splitSelectionIntoNewGroup() {
  622 |     const recordingId = recordingIdInput.trim();
  623 |     if (!recordingId) return;
  624 | 
  625 |     if (!lyrics || !groupMap) {
  626 |       setErr("Load a track first.");
  627 |       return;
  628 |     }
  629 |     if (!activeGroup || startsWithLk(activeGroup.key)) {
  630 |       setErr("Choose a canonical group first.");
  631 |       return;
  632 |     }
  633 |     if (selectedKeys.length === 0) {
  634 |       setErr("Select at least one line to split.");
  635 |       return;
  636 |     }
  637 | 
  638 |     const activeMembers = new Set<string>(activeGroup.lineKeys);
  639 |     const toSplit = selectedKeys.filter((lk) => activeMembers.has(lk));
  640 |     if (toSplit.length === 0) {
  641 |       setErr("Your selection has no lines from the active group.");
  642 |       return;
  643 |     }
  644 | 
  645 |     setErr("");
  646 |     setBusy(true);
  647 |     try {
  648 |       const newKey = await createNewGroupKey();
  649 |       await assignLineKeysToGroup(recordingId, newKey, toSplit);
  650 |       await loadGrouping(recordingId);
  651 |       setActiveGroupKey(newKey);
  652 |     } catch (e: unknown) {
  653 |       setErr(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Split failed.");
  654 |     } finally {
  655 |       setBusy(false);
  656 |     }
  657 |   }
  658 | 
  659 |   function selectActiveGroupLines() {
  660 |     if (!activeGroup) return;
  661 |     setSelectionForLineKeys(activeGroup.lineKeys, true);
  662 |   }
  663 | 
  664 |   function deselectActiveGroupLines() {
  665 |     if (!activeGroup) return;
  666 |     setSelectionForLineKeys(activeGroup.lineKeys, false);
  667 |   }
  668 | 
  669 |   const canAct = Boolean(lyrics && groupMap && loadedRecordingId);
  670 |   const hasSelection = selectedKeys.length > 0;
  671 | 
  672 |   return (
  673 |     <div className="rounded-xl bg-white/5 p-4">
  674 |       <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
  675 |         <div>
  676 |           <div className="text-sm opacity-70">Line grouping (Phase B1)</div>
  677 |           <div className="mt-1 text-xs opacity-60">
  678 |             Map multiple lineKeys → one canonical groupKey, then UI/comments
  679 |             resolve to the group.
  680 |           </div>
  681 |           <div className="mt-2 text-xs opacity-60">
  682 |             {stats.total} cues · {stats.mappedCount} mapped · {stats.unmapped}{" "}
  683 |             unmapped · {stats.canonCount} canonical groups
  684 |           </div>
  685 |         </div>
  686 | 
  687 |         <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
  688 |           <div className="flex flex-col items-end">
  689 |             <input
  690 |               list="exegesis-recordingId-catalogue"
  691 |               className="w-[360px] max-w-[56vw] rounded-md bg-black/20 px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none"
  692 |               placeholder="recordingId"
  693 |               value={recordingIdInput}
  694 |               onChange={(e) => setRecordingIdInput(e.target.value)}
  695 |               onKeyDown={(e) => {
  696 |                 if (e.key === "Enter") void loadGrouping();
  697 |               }}
  698 |             />
  699 |             <datalist id="exegesis-recordingId-catalogue">
  700 |               {knownRecordingIds.map((id) => (
  701 |                 <option key={id} value={id} />
  702 |               ))}
  703 |             </datalist>
  704 | 
  705 |             <div className="mt-1 text-[11px] opacity-60">
  706 |               {knownIdsBusy
  707 |                 ? "Loading catalogue…"
  708 |                 : knownIdsErr
  709 |                   ? `Catalogue unavailable: ${knownIdsErr}`
  710 |                   : knownRecordingIds.length
  711 |                     ? `${knownRecordingIds.length} known recordingIds`
  712 |                     : "No catalogue entries"}
  713 |             </div>
  714 |           </div>
  715 | 
  716 |           <button
  717 |             className="rounded-md bg-white/10 px-3 py-2 text-sm hover:bg-white/15 disabled:opacity-40"
  718 |             disabled={busy || !recordingIdInput.trim()}
  719 |             onClick={() => void loadGrouping()}
  720 |           >
  721 |             {busy ? "Loading…" : "Load"}
  722 |           </button>
  723 |         </div>
  724 |       </div>
  725 | 
  726 |       {err ? (
  727 |         <div className="mt-3 rounded-md bg-white/5 p-3 text-sm">{err}</div>
  728 |       ) : null}
  729 | 
  730 |       {lyrics ? (
  731 |         <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs opacity-60">
  732 |           <div>
  733 |             loaded: <span className="opacity-90">{lyrics.recordingId}</span>
  734 |           </div>
  735 |           <div>·</div>
  736 |           <div>lyrics v{lyrics.version}</div>
  737 |           {lyrics.geniusUrl ? (
  738 |             <>
  739 |               <div>·</div>
  740 |               <a
  741 |                 className="underline underline-offset-2 opacity-80 hover:opacity-95"
  742 |                 href={lyrics.geniusUrl}
  743 |                 target="_blank"
  744 |                 rel="noreferrer"
  745 |               >
  746 |                 genius
  747 |               </a>
  748 |             </>
  749 |           ) : null}
  750 |         </div>
  751 |       ) : null}
  752 | 
  753 |       <div className="mt-4 grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-[1.6fr_1fr]">
  754 |         {/* LEFT: cues */}
  755 |         <div className="rounded-xl bg-black/15 p-3">
  756 |           <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
  757 |             <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
  758 |               <div className="text-sm opacity-80">Lines</div>
  759 |               <div className="text-xs opacity-60">
  760 |                 {visibleCues.length} shown · {selectedKeys.length} selected
  761 |               </div>
  762 |             </div>
  763 | 
  764 |             <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
  765 |               <label className="flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 text-xs opacity-75">
  766 |                 <input
  767 |                   type="checkbox"
  768 |                   checked={showOnlyUnmapped}
  769 |                   onChange={(e) => setShowOnlyUnmapped(e.target.checked)}
  770 |                 />
  771 |                 Unmapped only
  772 |               </label>
  773 |               <label className="flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 text-xs opacity-75">
  774 |                 <input
  775 |                   type="checkbox"
  776 |                   checked={showOnlySelected}
  777 |                   onChange={(e) => setShowOnlySelected(e.target.checked)}
  778 |                 />
  779 |                 Selected only
  780 |               </label>
  781 | 
  782 |               <button
  783 |                 className="rounded-md bg-white/5 px-2 py-1 text-xs hover:bg-white/10 disabled:opacity-40"
  784 |                 disabled={!hasSelection}
  785 |                 onClick={() => clearSelection()}
  786 |                 title="Clear selection"
  787 |               >
  788 |                 Deselect all
  789 |               </button>
  790 |             </div>
  791 |           </div>
  792 | 
  793 |           <div
  794 |             className="mt-3 space-y-2"
  795 |             style={{ maxHeight: 560, overflowY: "auto" }}
  796 |           >
  797 |             {!lyrics ? (
  798 |               <div className="text-sm opacity-60">
  799 |                 Load a track to begin grouping.
  800 |               </div>
  801 |             ) : visibleCues.length === 0 ? (
  802 |               <div className="text-sm opacity-60">
  803 |                 No lines match the current filters.
  804 |               </div>
  805 |             ) : (
  806 |               visibleCues.map(({ cue: c, groupKey }, idx) => {
  807 |                 const isSel = Boolean(selectedLineKeys[c.lineKey]);
  808 |                 const isActiveGroup =
  809 |                   Boolean(activeGroupKey) && groupKey === activeGroupKey;
  810 |                 const isMappedCanonical = !startsWithLk(groupKey);
  811 | 
  812 |                 return (
  813 |                   <button
  814 |                     key={c.lineKey}
  815 |                     className={clsx([
  816 |                       "w-full rounded-md bg-black/20 p-3 text-left hover:bg-black/25",
  817 |                       isSel && "ring-1 ring-white/20",
  818 |                       isActiveGroup && "ring-1 ring-white/25",
  819 |                     ])}
  820 |                     onClick={(e) => onCueClick(idx, c.lineKey, e)}
  821 |                     title="Click to select/deselect. Shift-click for range."
  822 |                   >
  823 |                     <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
  824 |                       <div className="min-w-0">
  825 |                         <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs opacity-60">
  826 |                           <span className="opacity-70">{fmtTimeMs(c.tMs)}</span>
  827 |                           <span className="opacity-50">·</span>
  828 |                           <span className="truncate">{c.lineKey}</span>
  829 |                         </div>
  830 | 
  831 |                         <div className="mt-1 text-sm opacity-90 line-clamp-2">
  832 |                           {c.text}
  833 |                         </div>
  834 | 
  835 |                         <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
  836 |                           <button
  837 |                             type="button"
  838 |                             className={clsx([
  839 |                               "rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-xs",
  840 |                               isMappedCanonical
  841 |                                 ? "bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/15"
  842 |                                 : "bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/10",
  843 |                             ])}
  844 |                             onClick={(e) => {
  845 |                               e.stopPropagation();
  846 |                               setActiveGroupKey(groupKey);
  847 |                             }}
  848 |                             title="Focus this group"
  849 |                           >
  850 |                             <span className="opacity-80">group:</span>{" "}
  851 |                             <span className="opacity-95">{groupKey}</span>
  852 |                           </button>
  853 | 
  854 |                           {isMappedCanonical ? (
  855 |                             <span className="text-xs opacity-60">
  856 |                               canonical
  857 |                             </span>
  858 |                           ) : (
  859 |                             <span className="text-xs opacity-60">fallback</span>
  860 |                           )}
  861 |                         </div>
  862 |                       </div>
  863 | 
  864 |                       <div className="shrink-0 text-xs opacity-60">
  865 |                         {isSel ? "Selected" : ""}
  866 |                       </div>
  867 |                     </div>
  868 |                   </button>
  869 |                 );
  870 |               })
  871 |             )}
  872 |           </div>
  873 | 
  874 |           <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">

===== COMPLETE FILE: app/api/lyrics/by-track/route.ts =====
    1 | // web/app/api/lyrics/by-track/route.ts
    2 | import "server-only";
    3 | import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
    4 | import { client } from "@/sanity/lib/client";
    5 | import { sql } from "@vercel/postgres";
    6 | import { normalizeLyricCuesFromSanity } from "@/lib/types";
    7 | import type { LyricCue, LyricGroupMap } from "@/lib/types";
    8 | 
    9 | export const runtime = "nodejs";
   10 | 
   11 | type TrackLyricsDoc = {
   12 |   recordingId?: string;
   13 |   offsetMs?: number;
   14 |   version?: string;
   15 |   geniusUrl?: string | null;
   16 |   exegesisEnabled?: boolean;
   17 |   cues?: Array<{ _key?: string; tMs?: number; text?: string; endMs?: number }>;
   18 | };
   19 | 
   20 | type TrackMetaBundle = {
   21 |   albumTitle?: string | null;
   22 |   albumSlug?: string | null;
   23 |   albumCatalogueId?: string | null;
   24 |   track?: {
   25 |     title?: string | null;
   26 |     artist?: string | null;
   27 | 
   28 |     // canonical internal id
   29 |     recordingId?: string | null;
   30 | 
   31 |     // canonical URL id (per-album unique)
   32 |     displayId?: string | null;
   33 |   } | null;
   34 | };
   35 | 
   36 | type LyricsQueryResult = {
   37 |   lyrics?: TrackLyricsDoc | null;
   38 |   meta?: TrackMetaBundle | null;
   39 | };
   40 | 
   41 | function trimStr(v: unknown): string {
   42 |   return typeof v === "string" ? v.trim() : "";
   43 | }
   44 | 
   45 | function safeUrl(raw: unknown): string | null {
   46 |   const s = trimStr(raw);
   47 |   if (!s) return null;
   48 |   try {
   49 |     new URL(s);
   50 |     return s;
   51 |   } catch {
   52 |     return null;
   53 |   }
   54 | }
   55 | 
   56 | async function fetchGroupMap(recordingId: string): Promise<LyricGroupMap> {
   57 |   const r = await sql<{
   58 |     anchor_line_key: string;
   59 |     canonical_group_key: string;
   60 |     updated_at: string;
   61 |   }>`
   62 |     select anchor_line_key, canonical_group_key, updated_at
   63 |     from exegesis_group_map
   64 |     where track_id = ${recordingId}
   65 |   `;
   66 | 
   67 |   const map: LyricGroupMap = {};
   68 |   for (const row of r.rows ?? []) {
   69 |     const lk = trimStr(row.anchor_line_key);
   70 |     const gk = trimStr(row.canonical_group_key);
   71 |     if (!lk || !gk) continue;
   72 |     map[lk] = { canonicalGroupKey: gk, updatedAt: row.updated_at };
   73 |   }
   74 |   return map;
   75 | }
   76 | 
   77 | async function resolveRecordingIdFromAlbumDisplayId(args: {
   78 |   albumSlug: string;
   79 |   displayId: string;
   80 | }): Promise<{
   81 |   ok: true;
   82 |   recordingId: string;
   83 |   meta: TrackMetaBundle;
   84 | } | null> {
   85 |   const albumSlug = trimStr(args.albumSlug);
   86 |   const displayId = trimStr(args.displayId);
   87 |   if (!albumSlug || !displayId) return null;
   88 | 
   89 |   const q = `
   90 |     *[_type == "album" && slug.current == $albumSlug][0]{
   91 |       "albumTitle": title,
   92 |       "albumSlug": slug.current,
   93 |       "albumCatalogueId": catalogueId,
   94 |       "track": tracks[displayId == $displayId][0]{
   95 |         title,
   96 |         artist,
   97 |         recordingId,
   98 |         displayId
   99 |       }
  100 |     }
  101 |   `;
  102 | 
  103 |   const meta = await client.fetch<TrackMetaBundle | null>(q, {
  104 |     albumSlug,
  105 |     displayId,
  106 |   });
  107 | 
  108 |   const rec = trimStr(meta?.track?.recordingId);
  109 |   if (!rec) return null;
  110 | 
  111 |   return {
  112 |     ok: true,
  113 |     recordingId: rec,
  114 |     meta: meta ?? {
  115 |       albumTitle: null,
  116 |       albumSlug: albumSlug,
  117 |       albumCatalogueId: null,
  118 |       track: { title: null, artist: null, recordingId: rec, displayId },
  119 |     },
  120 |   };
  121 | }
  122 | 
  123 | export async function GET(req: Request) {
  124 |   const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);
  125 | 
  126 |   // Back-compat: allow direct internal-id lookups
  127 |   const recordingIdParam = trimStr(searchParams.get("recordingId"));
  128 | 
  129 |   // New canonical URL-facing surface: albumSlug + displayId
  130 |   const albumSlugParam = trimStr(searchParams.get("albumSlug"));
  131 |   const displayIdParam = trimStr(searchParams.get("displayId"));
  132 | 
  133 |   let recordingId = recordingIdParam;
  134 |   let forcedMeta: TrackMetaBundle | null = null;
  135 | 
  136 |   if (!recordingId) {
  137 |     const resolved = await resolveRecordingIdFromAlbumDisplayId({
  138 |       albumSlug: albumSlugParam,
  139 |       displayId: displayIdParam,
  140 |     });
  141 | 
  142 |     if (!resolved) {
  143 |       return NextResponse.json(
  144 |         {
  145 |           ok: false,
  146 |           error: "missing_recordingId_or_albumSlug_displayId",
  147 |         },
  148 |         { status: 400 },
  149 |       );
  150 |     }
  151 | 
  152 |     recordingId = resolved.recordingId;
  153 |     forcedMeta = resolved.meta;
  154 |   }
  155 | 
  156 |   if (recordingId.length > 200) {
  157 |     return NextResponse.json(
  158 |       { ok: false, error: "invalid_recordingId" },
  159 |       { status: 400 },
  160 |     );
  161 |   }
  162 | 
  163 |   // We always fetch lyrics by internal recordingId.
  164 |   // Meta is fetched either via (albumSlug+displayId) or a recordingId lookup.
  165 |   const q = `
  166 |     {
  167 |             "lyrics": *[_type == "lyrics" && recordingId == $recordingId][0]{
  168 |         recordingId,
  169 |         offsetMs,
  170 |         version,
  171 |         geniusUrl,
  172 |         exegesisEnabled,
  173 |         cues[]{ _key, tMs, text, endMs }
  174 |       },
  175 |       "meta": *[_type == "album" && $recordingId in tracks[].recordingId][0]{
  176 |         "albumTitle": title,
  177 |         "albumSlug": slug.current,
  178 |         "albumCatalogueId": catalogueId,
  179 |         "track": tracks[recordingId == $recordingId][0]{
  180 |           title,
  181 |           artist,
  182 |           recordingId,
  183 |           displayId
  184 |         }
  185 |       }
  186 |     }
  187 |   `;
  188 | 
  189 |   const bundle = await client.fetch<LyricsQueryResult | null>(q, {
  190 |     recordingId,
  191 |   });
  192 | 
  193 |   const doc = bundle?.lyrics ?? null;
  194 |   const metaRaw = forcedMeta ?? bundle?.meta ?? null;
  195 | 
  196 |   const trackTitle = trimStr(metaRaw?.track?.title) || null;
  197 |   const trackArtist = trimStr(metaRaw?.track?.artist) || null;
  198 | 
  199 |   const albumTitle = trimStr(metaRaw?.albumTitle) || null;
  200 |   const albumSlug = trimStr(metaRaw?.albumSlug) || null;
  201 |   const albumCatalogueId = trimStr(metaRaw?.albumCatalogueId) || null;
  202 | 
  203 |   const metaRecordingId = trimStr(metaRaw?.track?.recordingId) || null;
  204 |   const metaDisplayId = trimStr(metaRaw?.track?.displayId) || null;
  205 | 
  206 |   const cues = normalizeLyricCuesFromSanity(doc?.cues);
  207 |   const offsetMs =
  208 |     typeof doc?.offsetMs === "number" && Number.isFinite(doc.offsetMs)
  209 |       ? Math.floor(doc.offsetMs)
  210 |       : 0;
  211 | 
  212 |   const version = trimStr(doc?.version) || "v1";
  213 |   const geniusUrl = safeUrl(doc?.geniusUrl);
  214 |   const exegesisEnabled = doc?.exegesisEnabled !== false;
  215 | 
  216 |   // Embed exegesis grouping map (admin-auth not needed; it's just presentation data)
  217 |   const groupMap = await fetchGroupMap(recordingId);
  218 | 
  219 |   // Annotate cues with canonicalGroupKey when mapped (unmapped cues omit the field)
  220 |   const PARA_BREAK = "__PARA_BREAK__";
  221 | 
  222 |   const cuesWithGroups: LyricCue[] = cues.map((c) => {
  223 |     if (c.text === PARA_BREAK) return c; // never group-map paragraph breaks
  224 |     const hit = groupMap[c.lineKey];
  225 |     return hit ? { ...c, canonicalGroupKey: hit.canonicalGroupKey } : c;
  226 |   });
  227 | 
  228 |   return NextResponse.json(
  229 |     {
  230 |       ok: true,
  231 | 
  232 |       // canonical internal id (used for exegesis db + lyrics docs)
  233 |       recordingId,
  234 | 
  235 |       // canonical URL id when we can resolve it
  236 |       displayId: metaDisplayId,
  237 | 
  238 |       // meta (all nullable)
  239 |       trackTitle,
  240 |       trackArtist,
  241 |       albumTitle,
  242 |       albumSlug,
  243 |       albumCatalogueId,
  244 | 
  245 |       // explicit copies for callers that want them
  246 |       trackRecordingId: metaRecordingId,
  247 |       trackDisplayId: metaDisplayId,
  248 | 
  249 |       cues: cuesWithGroups,
  250 |       offsetMs,
  251 |       version,
  252 |       geniusUrl,
  253 |       exegesisEnabled,
  254 |       groupMap, // keyed by lineKey
  255 |     },
  256 |     {
  257 |       headers: {
  258 |         "Cache-Control": "no-store, max-age=0",
  259 |       },
  260 |     },
  261 |   );
  262 | }

   11 | type CatalogueOk = {
   12 |   ok: true;
   13 |   albums: Array<{
   14 |     albumId: string;
   15 |     albumSlug: string | null;
   16 |     albumTitle: string | null;
   17 |     coverUrl?: string | null; // ✅ add (source from same place as FullPlayer)
   18 |     recordingIds: string[]; // legacy
   19 |     tracks?: Array<{
   20 |       recordingId: string;
   21 |       displayId: string;
   22 |       title: string | null;
   23 |       artist: string | null;
   24 |       trackNo?: number | null; // optional; we can compute from index if absent

   26 |   }>;

   28 | type CatalogueErr = { ok: false; error: string };

   55 | function extractDisplayIdFromPath(pathname: string): string | null {
   56 |   // We only care about the canonical path segment, query is separate.
   57 |   // Expected: /exegesis or /exegesis/<displayId>
   58 |   const parts = (pathname ?? "")
   59 |     .split("?")[0]
   60 |     .split("#")[0]
   61 |     .split("/")
   62 |     .filter(Boolean);
   63 |   const idx = parts.indexOf("exegesis");
   64 |   if (idx < 0) return null;
   65 |   const next = parts[idx + 1] ?? "";
   66 |   const raw = decodeURIComponent(next).trim();
   67 |   return raw ? raw : null;
   68 | }

   70 | function buildCatalogueIndexes(cat: CatalogueOk | null): {
   71 |   recordingIdByDisplayId: Record<string, string>;
   72 |   trackMetaByRecordingId: Record<
   73 |     string,
   74 |     { title: string | null; artist: string | null; coverUrl: string | null }
   75 |   >;
   76 | } {
   77 |   const recordingIdByDisplayId: Record<string, string> = {};
   78 |   const trackMetaByRecordingId: Record<
   79 |     string,
   80 |     { title: string | null; artist: string | null; coverUrl: string | null }
   81 |   > = {};
   82 | 
   83 |   if (!cat) {
   84 |     return { recordingIdByDisplayId, trackMetaByRecordingId };
   85 |   }
   86 | 
   87 |   for (const album of cat.albums ?? []) {
   88 |     const albumCoverUrl = (album.coverUrl ?? "").trim() || null;
   89 | 
   90 |     for (const track of album.tracks ?? []) {
   91 |       const recordingId = (track.recordingId ?? "").trim();
   92 |       const displayId = (track.displayId ?? "").trim();
   93 |       if (!recordingId) continue;
   94 | 
   95 |       trackMetaByRecordingId[recordingId] = {
   96 |         title: track.title ?? null,
   97 |         artist: track.artist ?? null,
   98 |         coverUrl: albumCoverUrl,
   99 |       };
  100 | 
  101 |       if (displayId) {
  102 |         recordingIdByDisplayId[displayId] = recordingId;
  103 |       }
  104 |     }
  105 |   }
  106 | 
  107 |   return { recordingIdByDisplayId, trackMetaByRecordingId };
  108 | }

  110 | // ---- module-level caches (persist across route transitions) ----
  111 | let CATALOGUE_CACHE: CatalogueOk | null = null;
  112 | let CATALOGUE_PROMISE: Promise<CatalogueOk> | null = null;
  113 | 
  114 | function loadCatalogueCached(): Promise<CatalogueOk> {
  115 |   if (CATALOGUE_CACHE) return Promise.resolve(CATALOGUE_CACHE);
  116 |   if (CATALOGUE_PROMISE) return CATALOGUE_PROMISE;
  117 | 
  118 |   CATALOGUE_PROMISE = (async () => {
  119 |     const r = await fetch("/api/lyrics/catalogue", { cache: "no-store" });
  120 |     const j = (await r.json()) as CatalogueOk | CatalogueErr;
  121 |     if (!j.ok) throw new Error(j.error || "Failed to load catalogue.");
  122 |     CATALOGUE_CACHE = j;
  123 |     return j;
  124 |   })().finally(() => {
  125 |     CATALOGUE_PROMISE = null;
  126 |   });
  127 | 
  128 |   return CATALOGUE_PROMISE;
  129 | }
  130 | 
  131 | const TRACK_CACHE = new Map<string, LyricsOk>();
  132 | const TRACK_PROMISES = new Map<string, Promise<LyricsOk>>();
  133 | 
  134 | function loadTrackCached(tid: string): Promise<LyricsOk> {
  135 |   const key = tid.trim();
  136 |   if (!key) return Promise.reject(new Error("Missing recordingId"));
  137 | 
  138 |   const hit = TRACK_CACHE.get(key);
  139 |   if (hit) return Promise.resolve(hit);
  140 | 
  141 |   const inflight = TRACK_PROMISES.get(key);
  142 |   if (inflight) return inflight;
  143 | 
  144 |   const p = (async () => {
  145 |     const url = `/api/lyrics/by-track?recordingId=${encodeURIComponent(key)}`;
  146 |     const r = await fetch(url, { cache: "no-store" });
  147 |     const j = (await r.json()) as LyricsOk | LyricsErr;
  148 |     if (!j.ok) throw new Error(j.error || "Failed to load lyrics.");
  149 |     TRACK_CACHE.set(key, j);
  150 |     return j;
  151 |   })().finally(() => {
  152 |     TRACK_PROMISES.delete(key);
  153 |   });
  154 | 
  155 |   TRACK_PROMISES.set(key, p);
  156 |   return p;
  157 | }
  158 | 
  159 | function prefetchTrack(tid: string) {
  160 |   // Fire-and-forget warm-up
  161 |   void loadTrackCached(tid).catch(() => {});
  162 | }
  163 | 
  164 | function getCachedTrack(tid: string): LyricsOk | null {
  165 |   const key = (tid ?? "").trim();
  166 |   if (!key) return null;
  167 |   return TRACK_CACHE.get(key) ?? null;
  168 | }

  178 | // ---- cover tint cache (module scope) ----
  179 | const COVER_TINT_CACHE = new Map<string, string>(); // url -> "rgba(r,g,b,a)"
  180 | const COVER_TINT_INFLIGHT = new Map<string, Promise<string | null>>();
  181 | 
  182 | function clamp255(n: number) {
  183 |   return Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(n)));
  184 | }
  185 | 
  186 | function computeAverageRgb(data: Uint8ClampedArray) {
  187 |   let r = 0;
  188 |   let g = 0;
  189 |   let b = 0;
  190 |   let count = 0;
  191 | 
  192 |   for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i += 4) {
  193 |     const a = data[i + 3] ?? 0;
  194 |     if (a < 16) continue;
  195 | 
  196 |     const rr = data[i] ?? 0;
  197 |     const gg = data[i + 1] ?? 0;
  198 |     const bb = data[i + 2] ?? 0;
  199 | 
  200 |     if (rr + gg + bb < 36) continue;
  201 | 
  202 |     r += rr;
  203 |     g += gg;
  204 |     b += bb;
  205 |     count++;
  206 |   }
  207 | 
  208 |   if (!count) return null;
  209 | 
  210 |   r /= count;
  211 |   g /= count;
  212 |   b /= count;
  213 | 
  214 |   // lift slightly for visibility
  215 |   const base = {
  216 |     r: clamp255(r + 18),
  217 |     g: clamp255(g + 18),
  218 |     b: clamp255(b + 18),
  219 |   };
  220 | 
  221 |   // darker companion tone
  222 |   const dark = {
  223 |     r: clamp255(base.r * 0.65),
  224 |     g: clamp255(base.g * 0.65),
  225 |     b: clamp255(base.b * 0.65),
  226 |   };
  227 | 
  228 |   return { base, dark };
  229 | }
  230 | 
  231 | async function extractCoverTint(url: string): Promise<string | null> {
  232 |   const key = (url ?? "").trim();
  233 |   if (!key) return null;
  234 | 
  235 |   const cached = COVER_TINT_CACHE.get(key);
  236 |   if (cached) return cached;
  237 | 
  238 |   const inflight = COVER_TINT_INFLIGHT.get(key);
  239 |   if (inflight) return inflight;
  240 | 
  241 |   const p = (async () => {
  242 |     try {
  243 |       const img = new Image();
  244 |       img.crossOrigin = "anonymous";
  245 |       img.decoding = "async";
  246 | 
  247 |       const loaded = new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
  248 |         img.onload = () => resolve();
  249 |         img.onerror = () => reject(new Error("img_load_failed"));
  250 |       });
  251 | 
  252 |       img.src = key;
  253 |       await loaded;
  254 | 
  255 |       // downsample hard for speed
  256 |       const size = 36;
  257 |       const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
  258 |       canvas.width = size;
  259 |       canvas.height = size;
  260 | 
  261 |       const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d", { willReadFrequently: true });
  262 |       if (!ctx) return null;
  263 | 
  264 |       ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, size, size);
  265 |       const { data } = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, size, size);
  266 | 
  267 |       const avg = computeAverageRgb(data);
  268 |       if (!avg) return null;
  269 | 
  270 |       const base = `rgba(${avg.base.r}, ${avg.base.g}, ${avg.base.b}, 0.45)`;
  271 |       const dark = `rgba(${avg.dark.r}, ${avg.dark.g}, ${avg.dark.b}, 0.45)`;
  272 | 
  273 |       const gradient = `linear-gradient(135deg, ${base}, ${dark})`;
  274 |       COVER_TINT_CACHE.set(key, gradient);
  275 |       return gradient;
  276 |     } catch {
  277 |       return null;
  278 |     } finally {
  279 |       COVER_TINT_INFLIGHT.delete(key);
  280 |     }
  281 |   })();
  282 | 
  283 |   COVER_TINT_INFLIGHT.set(key, p);
  284 |   const out = await p;
  285 |   return out;
  286 | }

  288 | function AlbumCard(props: {
  289 |   a: CatalogueOk["albums"][number];
  290 |   label: string;
  291 |   search: string;
  292 |   onOpenTrack: (
  293 |     event: React.MouseEvent<HTMLAnchorElement>,
  294 |     displayId: string,
  295 |     recordingId: string,
  296 |   ) => void;
  297 |   onPrefetchRoute: (href: string) => void;
  298 | }) {
  299 |   const { a, label, search, onOpenTrack, onPrefetchRoute } = props;
  300 | 
  301 |   const [tint, setTint] = React.useState<string | null>(null);
  302 | 
  303 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  304 |     const url = (a.coverUrl ?? "").trim();
  305 |     if (!url) {
  306 |       setTint(null);
  307 |       return;
  308 |     }
  309 | 
  310 |     let alive = true;
  311 | 
  312 |     void extractCoverTint(url).then((c) => {
  313 |       if (!alive) return;
  314 |       setTint(c);
  315 |     });
  316 | 
  317 |     return () => {
  318 |       alive = false;
  319 |     };
  320 |   }, [a.coverUrl]);
  321 | 
  322 |   // Use tint as border + subtle glow
  323 |   const borderGradient = tint ?? "rgba(255,255,255,0.10)";
  324 |   const glowCol = tint
  325 |     ? borderGradient.replace(/0\.45/g, "0.18")
  326 |     : "rgba(255,255,255,0.06)";
  327 |

  329 |     <div
  330 |       className="rounded-xl bg-white/5 p-4"
  331 |       style={{
  332 |         border: "1px solid transparent",
  333 |         backgroundClip: "padding-box",

  335 |         position: "relative",
  336 |       }}
  337 |     >
  338 |       <div
  339 |         className="absolute inset-0 rounded-xl pointer-events-none"
  340 |         style={{
  341 |           padding: 1,
  342 |           background: borderGradient,
  343 |           WebkitMask:
  344 |             "linear-gradient(#000 0 0) content-box, linear-gradient(#000 0 0)",
  345 |           WebkitMaskComposite: "xor",
  346 |           maskComposite: "exclude",
  347 |         }}
  348 |       />
  349 |       {/* Album hero (full-bleed to card edges despite card padding) */}
  350 |       <div className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-t-[11px] -mx-4 -mt-4 mb-4 m-[1px]">
  351 |         {/* Background texture */}
  352 |         {a.coverUrl ? (
  353 |           <div
  354 |             className="absolute inset-0 scale-110"
  355 |             style={{
  356 |               backgroundImage: `url(${a.coverUrl})`,
  357 |               backgroundPosition: "center",
  358 |               backgroundSize: "cover",
  359 |               opacity: 0.22, // <-- tweak 0.14–0.30
  360 |             }}
  361 |             aria-hidden="true"
  362 |           />
  363 |         ) : null}
  364 | 
  365 |         {/* Optional: extremely subtle legibility wash (remove entirely if you want pure artwork) */}
  366 |         <div
  367 |           className="absolute inset-0"
  368 |           style={{
  369 |             background:
  370 |               "linear-gradient(to bottom, rgba(0,0,0,0.10), rgba(0,0,0,0.35))", // <-- tweak 0.00–0.18 and 0.20–0.45
  371 |           }}
  372 |           aria-hidden="true"
  373 |         />
  374 | 
  375 |         {/* Foreground content (padding lives here now) */}
  376 |         <div className="relative flex items-center gap-5 px-5 py-6">
  377 |           {/* Large artwork */}
  378 |           <div
  379 |             className="w-1/3 aspect-square shrink-0 rounded-md shadow-lg"
  380 |             style={{
  381 |               border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.10)",
  382 |               background: a.coverUrl
  383 |                 ? `url(${a.coverUrl}) center/cover no-repeat`
  384 |                 : undefined,
  385 |             }}

  388 |

  392 |               {label}
  393 |             </div>
  394 |           </div>
  395 |         </div>
  396 |       </div>
  397 | 
  398 |       <div className="mt-4 space-y-2">
  399 |         {(a.tracks ?? []).map((t, i) => {
  400 |           const tid = (t.recordingId ?? "").trim();
  401 |           const displayId = (t.displayId ?? "").trim();
  402 |           if (!tid || !displayId) return null;
  403 | 
  404 |           const trackLabel = (t.title ?? "").trim() || displayId;

  406 |             typeof t.trackNo === "number" && t.trackNo > 0 ? t.trackNo : i + 1;
  407 |

  413 |                 prefetchTrack(tid);

  416 |               onFocus={() => {

  420 |               onMouseDown={() => {
  421 |                 prefetchTrack(tid);

  424 |               onTouchStart={() => {

  443 |       </div>
  444 |     </div>

  448 | export default function PortalExegesis(props: { title?: string }) {
  449 |   const pathname = usePathname() ?? "";
  450 |   const router = useRouter();
  451 |   const sp = useSearchParams();
  452 |   const search = sp?.toString() ? `?${sp.toString()}` : "";
  453 | 
  454 |   const { exegesisDisplayId, setExegesisDisplayId } = usePortalViewer();
  455 |   const [optimisticDisplayId, setOptimisticDisplayId] = React.useState<
  456 |     string | null | undefined

   10 | import { mediaSurface } from "./mediaSurface";
   11 | import { useLyricsSnapshot } from "./lyrics/useLyricsSurface";
   12 | 
   13 | type CuesByRecordingId = Record<string, LyricCue[]>;
   14 | type OffsetByRecordingId = Record<string, number>;
   15 | 
   16 | function pickKeyWithCues(
   17 |   cuesByRecordingId: CuesByRecordingId | undefined,
   18 |   keys: Array<string | null | undefined>,
   19 | ) {
   20 |   if (!cuesByRecordingId) return (keys.find(Boolean) as string | null) ?? null;
   21 |   for (const k of keys) {
   22 |     if (!k) continue;
   23 |     const xs = cuesByRecordingId[k];
   24 |     if (Array.isArray(xs) && xs.length) return k;
   25 |   }
   26 |   return (keys.find(Boolean) as string | null) ?? null;
   27 | }
   28 | 
   29 | function StageCore(props: {
   30 |   variant: "inline" | "fullscreen";
   31 |   cuesByRecordingId?: CuesByRecordingId;
   32 |   offsetByRecordingId?: OffsetByRecordingId;
   33 |   offsetMs?: number;
   34 |   autoResumeOnSeek?: boolean;
   35 |   lyricsMode?: "embedded" | "none";
   36 | }) {
   37 |   const {
   38 |     variant,
   39 |     cuesByRecordingId: cuesByRecordingIdProp,
   40 |     offsetByRecordingId: offsetByRecordingIdProp,
   41 |     offsetMs: globalOffsetMs = 0,
   42 |     autoResumeOnSeek = false,
   43 |     lyricsMode = "embedded",
   44 |   } = props;
   45 | 
   46 |   const player = usePlayerVisual();
   47 |   const actions = usePlayerActions();
   48 |   const snap = useLyricsSnapshot();
   49 | 
   50 |   // ✅ prefer props when provided; otherwise use lyricsSurface
   51 |   const cuesByRecordingId = cuesByRecordingIdProp ?? snap.cuesByRecordingId;
   52 |   const offsetByRecordingId =
   53 |     offsetByRecordingIdProp ?? snap.offsetByRecordingId;
   54 | 
   55 |   // Register stage presence; fullscreen wins if it exists.
   56 |   React.useEffect(() => mediaSurface.registerStage(variant), [variant]);
   57 | 
   58 |   const [surfaceRecordingId, setSurfaceRecordingId] = React.useState<
   59 |     string | null
   60 |   >(() => mediaSurface.getRecordingId());
   61 | 
   62 |   React.useEffect(() => {
   63 |     const unsub = mediaSurface.subscribe((e) => {
   64 |       if (e.type === "track") setSurfaceRecordingId(e.id);
   65 |     });
   66 |     return unsub;
   67 |   }, []);
   68 | 
   69 |   const playerRecordingId = player.current?.recordingId ?? null;
   70 |   const playerDisplayId = player.current?.displayId ?? null;
   71 |   const playerMuxId = player.current?.muxPlaybackId ?? null;
   72 | 
   73 |   const recordingId = pickKeyWithCues(cuesByRecordingId, [
   74 |     playerRecordingId,
   75 |     surfaceRecordingId,
   76 |     playerMuxId,
   77 |   ]);
   78 | 
   79 |   const displayId = playerDisplayId;
   80 | 
   81 |   const cues: LyricCue[] | null = recordingId
   82 |     ? Array.isArray(cuesByRecordingId?.[recordingId]) &&
   83 |       cuesByRecordingId[recordingId].length
   84 |       ? cuesByRecordingId[recordingId]
   85 |       : null
   86 |     : null;
   87 | 
   88 |   const trackOffsetMs =
   89 |     recordingId && typeof offsetByRecordingId?.[recordingId] === "number"
   90 |       ? Number.isFinite(offsetByRecordingId[recordingId])
   91 |         ? offsetByRecordingId[recordingId]
   92 |         : 0
   93 |       : 0;
   94 | 
   95 |   const effectiveOffsetMs = trackOffsetMs + globalOffsetMs;
   96 |   const resumeTrack = player.current ?? player.firstQueuedTrack;
   97 | 
   98 |   const onSeek = React.useCallback(
   99 |     (tMs: number) => {
  100 |       const ms = Math.max(0, Math.floor(tMs));
  101 |       actions.seek(ms);
  102 | 
  103 |       if (autoResumeOnSeek) {
  104 |         if (!resumeTrack) return;
  105 |         actions.setIntent("play");
  106 |         actions.play(resumeTrack);
  107 |         window.dispatchEvent(new Event("af:play-intent"));
  108 |       }
  109 |     },
  110 |     [actions, autoResumeOnSeek, resumeTrack],
  111 |   );
  112 | 
  113 |   const lyricsVariant = variant === "inline" ? "inline" : "stage";
  114 |   const reservedBottomPx = variant === "inline" ? 0 : STAGE_TRANSPORT_FOOTER_PX;
  115 | 
  116 |   return (
  117 |     <div
  118 |       style={{
  119 |         position: "absolute",
  120 |         inset: 0,
  121 |         borderRadius: 18,
  122 |         overflow: "hidden",
  123 |         background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.35)",
  124 |         isolation: "isolate",
  125 |       }}
  126 |     >
  127 |       <div style={{ position: "absolute", inset: 0, zIndex: 0 }}>
  128 |         <VisualizerCanvas variant={variant} />
  129 |       </div>

  132 |         aria-hidden="true"

  138 |             variant === "inline"

  140 |               : "radial-gradient(70% 55% at 50% 40%, rgba(0,0,0,0.00), rgba(0,0,0,0.28) 72%), linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(0,0,0,0.22), rgba(0,0,0,0.10) 45%, rgba(0,0,0,0.24))",
  141 |           pointerEvents: "none",

  148 |             recordingId={recordingId}
  149 |             displayId={displayId}
  150 |             cues={cues}
  151 |             offsetMs={effectiveOffsetMs}
  152 |             onSeek={onSeek}
  153 |             variant={lyricsVariant}
  154 |             reservedBottomPx={reservedBottomPx}
  155 |           />
  156 |         ) : null}
  157 |       </div>
  158 |     </div>
  159 |   );
  160 | }

   13 | function lockBodyScroll(lock: boolean) {
   14 |   if (typeof document === "undefined") return;
   15 |   const el = document.documentElement;
   16 |   const body = document.body;
   17 |   if (lock) {
   18 |     el.style.overflow = "hidden";
   19 |     body.style.overflow = "hidden";
   20 |     body.style.touchAction = "none";
   21 |   } else {
   22 |     el.style.overflow = "";
   23 |     body.style.overflow = "";
   24 |     body.style.touchAction = "";
   25 |   }
   26 | }
   27 | 
   28 | function useIsMobile(breakpointPx = 640) {
   29 |   const [isMobile, setIsMobile] = React.useState(false);
   30 | 
   31 |   React.useEffect(() => {
   32 |     const mq = window.matchMedia(`(max-width: ${breakpointPx}px)`);
   33 |     const apply = () => setIsMobile(mq.matches);
   34 |     apply();
   35 |     mq.addEventListener?.("change", apply);
   36 |     return () => mq.removeEventListener?.("change", apply);
   37 |   }, [breakpointPx]);
   38 | 
   39 |   return isMobile;
   40 | }
   41 | 
   42 | function useIsAdminBodyFlag() {
   43 |   const [isAdmin, setIsAdmin] = React.useState(false);
   44 | 
   45 |   React.useEffect(() => {
   46 |     if (typeof document === "undefined") return;
   47 | 
   48 |     const body = document.body;
   49 |     const apply = () => setIsAdmin(body.dataset.afIsAdmin === "1");
   50 | 
   51 |     apply();
   52 | 
   53 |     const mo = new MutationObserver(() => apply());
   54 |     mo.observe(body, {
   55 |       attributes: true,
   56 |       attributeFilter: ["data-af-is-admin"],
   57 |     });
   58 | 
   59 |     return () => mo.disconnect();
   60 |   }, []);
   61 | 
   62 |   return isAdmin;
   63 | }

   65 | function useIdleCursor(active: boolean, timeoutMs: number) {
   66 |   const [hidden, setHidden] = React.useState(false);
   67 | 
   68 |   React.useEffect(() => {
   69 |     if (!active) {
   70 |       setHidden(false);
   71 |       return;
   72 |     }
   73 | 
   74 |     let timer: number | null = null;
   75 | 
   76 |     const reset = () => {
   77 |       setHidden(false);
   78 |       if (timer != null) window.clearTimeout(timer);
   79 |       timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
   80 |         setHidden(true);
   81 |       }, timeoutMs);
   82 |     };
   83 | 
   84 |     reset();
   85 | 
   86 |     const onMove = () => reset();
   87 |     const onDown = () => reset();
   88 | 
   89 |     window.addEventListener("mousemove", onMove, { passive: true });
   90 |     window.addEventListener("mousedown", onDown, { passive: true });
   91 | 
   92 |     return () => {
   93 |       if (timer != null) window.clearTimeout(timer);
   94 |       window.removeEventListener("mousemove", onMove);
   95 |       window.removeEventListener("mousedown", onDown);
   96 |     };
   97 |   }, [active, timeoutMs]);
   98 | 
   99 |   return hidden;
  100 | }

  291 | export default function StageInline(props: { height?: number }) {
  292 |   const { height = 300 } = props;
  293 |   const p = usePlayer();
  294 | 
  295 |   // Lazy-load lyrics for the currently playing track when missing.
  296 |   const currentRecordingId = p.current?.recordingId ?? null;
  297 | 
  298 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  299 |     if (!currentRecordingId) return;
  300 | 
  301 |     const recordingId = currentRecordingId; // stable capture
  302 |     const ac = new AbortController();
  303 | 
  304 |     void ensureLyricsForTrack(recordingId, { signal: ac.signal });
  305 | 
  306 |     return () => ac.abort();
  307 |   }, [currentRecordingId]);
  308 | 
  309 |   const isMobile = useIsMobile(640);
  310 |   const isAdmin = useIsAdminBodyFlag();
  311 |   const inlineHeight = isMobile
  312 |     ? Math.max(140, Math.round(height * 0.5))
  313 |     : height;
  314 | 
  315 |   const [mounted, setMounted] = React.useState(false);
  316 |   React.useEffect(() => setMounted(true), []);
  317 | 
  318 |   const [open, setOpen] = React.useState(false);
  319 |   const enableIdleCursor = open && !isMobile;
  320 |   const cursorHidden = useIdleCursor(enableIdleCursor, 3000);
  321 | 
  322 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  323 |     lockBodyScroll(open);
  324 |     return () => lockBodyScroll(false);
  325 |   }, [open]);
  326 | 
  327 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  328 |     if (!open) return;
  329 |     if (typeof document === "undefined") return;
  330 | 
  331 |     const exitFsIfNeeded = async () => {
  332 |       try {
  333 |         if (
  334 |           document.fullscreenElement &&
  335 |           typeof document.exitFullscreen === "function"
  336 |         ) {
  337 |           await document.exitFullscreen();
  338 |         }
  339 |       } catch {
  340 |         // ignore
  341 |       }
  342 |     };
  343 | 
  344 |     const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
  345 |       if (e.key !== "Escape") return;
  346 |       e.preventDefault();
  347 |       void exitFsIfNeeded().finally(() => setOpen(false));
  348 |     };
  349 | 
  350 |     const onFsChange = () => {
  351 |       if (!document.fullscreenElement) setOpen(false);
  352 |     };
  353 | 
  354 |     window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  355 |     document.addEventListener("fullscreenchange", onFsChange);
  356 |     return () => {
  357 |       window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  358 |       document.removeEventListener("fullscreenchange", onFsChange);
  359 |     };
  360 |   }, [open]);
  361 | 
  362 |   const tryRequestFullscreen = React.useCallback(async () => {
  363 |     if (typeof document === "undefined") return;
  364 |     const el = document.getElementById("af-stage-overlay");
  365 |     if (!el) return;
  366 |     const requestFullscreen = (
  367 |       el as Element & { requestFullscreen?: () => Promise<void> }
  368 |     ).requestFullscreen;
  369 |     if (typeof requestFullscreen !== "function") return;
  370 |     try {
  371 |       await requestFullscreen.call(el);
  372 |     } catch {
  373 |       // ignore
  374 |     }
  375 |   }, []);
  376 | 
  377 |   const nothingPlaying = p.queue.length === 0 && !p.current?.recordingId;
  378 | 
  379 |   const handleOverlayBackdropMouseDown = React.useCallback(
  380 |     (e: React.MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
  381 |       if (e.target === e.currentTarget) setOpen(false);
  382 |     },
  383 |     [],
  384 |   );
  385 | 
  386 |   const handleOverlayClose = React.useCallback(() => {
  387 |     setOpen(false);
  388 |   }, []);
  389 | 
  390 |   const handleOverlayRequestFullscreen = React.useCallback(() => {
  391 |     void tryRequestFullscreen();
  392 |   }, [tryRequestFullscreen]);
  393 | 
  394 |   const overlay =
  395 |     mounted && open
  396 |       ? createPortal(
  397 |           <FullscreenStageOverlay
  398 |             cursorHidden={cursorHidden}
  399 |             isMobile={isMobile}
  400 |             showPerfHud={isAdmin}
  401 |             onBackdropMouseDown={handleOverlayBackdropMouseDown}
  402 |             onRequestFullscreen={handleOverlayRequestFullscreen}
  403 |             onClose={handleOverlayClose}
  404 |           />,
  405 |           document.body,
  406 |         )
  407 |       : null;
  408 | 
  409 |   return (
  410 |     <>
  411 |       <div
  412 |         style={{
  413 |           borderRadius: 18,
  414 |           border: "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.12)",
  415 |           background: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)",
  416 |           overflow: "hidden",
  417 |           height: inlineHeight,
  418 |           position: "relative",
  419 |         }}
  420 |       >
  421 |         {!open ? (
  422 |           <div style={{ position: "absolute", inset: 0 }}>
  423 |             <StageCore variant="inline" lyricsMode="embedded" />
  424 |           </div>
  425 |         ) : null}
  426 | 
  427 |         <div
  428 |           aria-hidden
  429 |           style={{
  430 |             position: "absolute",
  431 |             left: 0,
  432 |             right: 0,
  433 |             top: 0,
  434 |             height: 56,
  435 |             background:
  436 |               "linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(0,0,0,0.45), rgba(0,0,0,0.18) 55%, rgba(0,0,0,0.00))",
  437 |             pointerEvents: "none",
  438 |           }}
  439 |         />
  440 | 
  441 |         <div
  442 |           style={{
  443 |             position: "absolute",
  444 |             top: 10,
  445 |             right: 10,
  446 |             pointerEvents: "auto",
  447 |             zIndex: 50,
  448 |           }}
  449 |         >
  450 |           <RoundIconButton
  451 |             label="Open stage fullscreen"
  452 |             title={nothingPlaying ? "Nothing playing" : "Open fullscreen stage"}
  453 |             disabled={nothingPlaying}
  454 |             onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
  455 |           >
  456 |             <IconFullscreen />
  457 |           </RoundIconButton>
  458 |         </div>
  459 |       </div>
  460 | 
  461 |       {overlay}
  462 |     </>
  463 |   );
  464 | }

    2 | "use client";
    3 | 
    4 | import React from "react";
    5 | import { createPortal } from "react-dom";
    6 | import StageInline from "@/app/home/player/StageInline";
