    8 | type SlotConfig = {
    9 |   height: number;
   10 | };
   11 | 
   12 | function safeParseHeight(v: string | null | undefined, fallback: number) {
   13 |   const n = v ? Number(v) : NaN;
   14 |   return Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0 ? n : fallback;
   15 | }
   16 | 
   17 | function ensureOffscreenParking(): HTMLElement {
   18 |   const existing = document.getElementById("af-stage-inline-offscreen");
   19 |   if (existing) return existing;
   20 | 
   21 |   const el = document.createElement("div");
   22 |   el.id = "af-stage-inline-offscreen";
   23 |   el.style.position = "fixed";
   24 |   el.style.left = "-100000px";
   25 |   el.style.top = "0";
   26 | 
   27 |   // IMPORTANT: do NOT shrink to 1px.
   28 |   // Keep a stable, “real” box so WebGL/canvas code doesn’t treat it as effectively unmounted.
   29 |   el.style.width = "720px";
   30 |   el.style.height = "560px";
   31 | 
   32 |   el.style.overflow = "hidden";
   33 |   el.style.pointerEvents = "none";
   34 |   el.style.opacity = "0";
   35 |   el.style.contain = "layout paint size";
   36 |   document.body.appendChild(el);
   37 |   return el;
   38 | }
   39 | 
   40 | /**
   41 |  * A single stable host element that React portals into forever.
   42 |  * We physically move this element into the current slot (if present),
   43 |  * otherwise park it offscreen. Moving the DOM node does NOT remount React.
   44 |  */
   45 | function ensureStableHostEl(): HTMLElement {
   46 |   const existing = document.getElementById("af-stage-inline-host");
   47 |   if (existing) return existing;
   48 | 
   49 |   const el = document.createElement("div");
   50 |   el.id = "af-stage-inline-host";
   51 |   el.style.width = "100%";
   52 |   el.style.height = "100%";
   53 |   // Important: do not position here; it inherits context from the slot container.
   54 |   return el;
   55 | }
   56 | 
   57 | function readSlotConfig(
   58 |   slot: HTMLElement | null,
   59 |   fallback: SlotConfig,
   60 | ): SlotConfig {
   61 |   if (!slot) return fallback;
   62 | 
   63 |   const height = safeParseHeight(
   64 |     slot.getAttribute("data-height"),
   65 |     fallback.height,
   66 |   );
   67 |   return { height };
   68 | }
   69 | 
   70 | function dbgEnabled(): boolean {
   71 |   try {
   72 |     return window.sessionStorage.getItem("af_dbg_stage_host") === "1";
   73 |   } catch {
   74 |     return false;
   75 |   }
   76 | }
   77 | 
   78 | function dbg(...args: unknown[]) {
   79 |   if (!dbgEnabled()) return;
   80 |   console.log("[StageInlineHost]", ...args);
   81 | }
   82 | 
   83 | export default function StageInlineHost(props: {
   84 |   /** Optional defaults; layouts can override via slot data-* attrs */
   85 |   height?: number;
   86 |   /** Slot id to attach the host into when present */
   87 |   slotId?: string;
   88 | }) {
   89 |   const slotId = props.slotId ?? "af-stage-inline-slot";
   90 | 
   91 |   const fallback = React.useMemo<SlotConfig>(
   92 |     () => ({ height: props.height ?? 560 }),
   93 |     [props.height],
   94 |   );
   95 | 
   96 |   // Important: first client render must match the server render.
   97 |   // So we start with null and only create the host after mount.
   98 |   const [hostEl, setHostEl] = React.useState<HTMLElement | null>(null);
   99 | 
  100 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  101 |     setHostEl(ensureStableHostEl());
  102 |   }, []);
  103 | 
  104 |   // Config is stateful (allowed to change), but portal container is NOT.
  105 |   const [cfg, setCfg] = React.useState<SlotConfig>(fallback);
  106 | 
  107 |   // Every session layout renders the inline-stage slot. Attach during hydration
  108 |   // and once again on the next paint, rather than permanently observing every
  109 |   // mutation beneath document.body for a slot that does not disappear.
  110 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  111 |     if (!hostEl) return;
  112 | 
  113 |     const parking = ensureOffscreenParking();
  114 |     let cancelled = false;
  115 |     let retryFrame: number | null = null;
  116 | 
  117 |     const attach = () => {
  118 |       if (cancelled) return;
  119 | 
  120 |       const slot = document.getElementById(slotId) as HTMLElement | null;
  121 |       const targetParent = slot ?? parking;
  122 | 
  123 |       // Move hostEl only if its parent actually changed.
  124 |       if (hostEl.parentElement !== targetParent) {
  125 |         try {
  126 |           targetParent.appendChild(hostEl);
  127 |           dbg(
  128 |             "moved hostEl into",
  129 |             slot ? `#${slotId}` : "#af-stage-inline-offscreen",
  130 |           );
  131 |         } catch (error) {
  132 |           dbg("appendChild failed", error);
  133 |         }
  134 |       }
  135 | 
  136 |       const nextCfg = readSlotConfig(slot, fallback);
  137 | 
  138 |       // Keep the fallback parking box sized correctly even though ordinary
  139 |       // session layouts should keep the host in the visible stage slot.
  140 |       parking.style.height = `${Math.max(1, Math.floor(nextCfg.height))}px`;
  141 | 
  142 |       setCfg((previous) =>
  143 |         previous.height === nextCfg.height ? previous : nextCfg,
  144 |       );
  145 |     };
  146 | 
  147 |     // The microtask covers ordinary hydration; the next-frame retry covers
  148 |     // any late sibling commit without keeping a permanent global observer.
  149 |     queueMicrotask(attach);
  150 |     retryFrame = window.requestAnimationFrame(attach);
  151 | 
  152 |     return () => {
  153 |       cancelled = true;
  154 | 
  155 |       if (retryFrame != null) {
  156 |         window.cancelAnimationFrame(retryFrame);
  157 |       }
  158 | 
  159 |       // Do NOT remove hostEl; preserving it retains stage and WebGL state.
  160 |     };
  161 |   }, [hostEl, slotId, fallback]);
  162 | 
  163 |   if (!hostEl) return null;
  164 | 
  165 |   // StageInline now owns its own data source (lyricsSurface + fetch), host only provides stable mount + height.
  166 |   return createPortal(<StageInline height={cfg.height} />, hostEl);
  167 | }

  111 | export default function StageTransportBar() {
  112 |   const p = usePlayer();
  113 | 
  114 |   const playingish =
  115 |     p.status === "playing" ||
  116 |     p.status === "loading" ||
  117 |     p.intent === "play" ||
  118 |     p.playRequestPending;
  119 | 
  120 |   const curId = p.current?.recordingId ?? "";
  121 |   const idx = curId ? p.queue.findIndex((t) => t.recordingId === curId) : -1;
  122 |   const atStart = idx <= 0;
  123 |   const atEnd = idx >= 0 && idx === p.queue.length - 1;
  124 | 
  125 |   const [transportLock, setTransportLock] = React.useState(false);
  126 |   const lockFor = (ms: number) => {
  127 |     setTransportLock(true);
  128 |     window.setTimeout(() => setTransportLock(false), ms);
  129 |   };
  130 | 
  131 |   const prevDisabled = !p.current || transportLock || atStart;
  132 |   const nextDisabled = !p.current || transportLock || atEnd;
  133 |   const playDisabled = (!p.current && p.queue.length === 0) || transportLock;
  134 | 
  135 |   return (
  136 |     <div
  137 |       data-af-stage-transport

  139 |         position: "absolute",
  140 |         left: 0,
  141 |         right: 0,
  142 |         bottom: 0,

  144 |

  147 |

  149 |         paddingRight: `calc(14px + env(safe-area-inset-right, 0px))`,
  150 |         paddingBottom: `calc(14px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px))`,
  151 |         paddingTop: 12,
  152 |

===== COMPLETE FILE: app/home/player/lyrics/ensureLyricsForTrack.ts =====
    1 | // web/app/home/player/lyrics/ensureLyricsForTrack.ts
    2 | "use client";
    3 | 
    4 | import { lyricsSurface } from "./lyricsSurface";
    5 | import { fetchLyricsByrecordingId } from "./fetchLyricsByRecordingId";
    6 | import type { AlbumLyricsBundle } from "@/lib/types";
    7 | 
    8 | // Module-scope in-flight map so *any* caller (any surface) dedupes fetches.
    9 | const inFlight = new Map<string, AbortController>();
   10 | 
   11 | function hasOwn(obj: object, key: string): boolean {
   12 |   return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(obj, key);
   13 | }
   14 | 
   15 | /**
   16 |  * Prime lyricsSurface from an album-provided bundle.
   17 |  * - Merges without clobbering existing per-track entries (unless missing).
   18 |  * - Ensures we still preserve "known no lyrics" as [] if already present.
   19 |  * - No network; safe to call repeatedly (idempotent-ish).
   20 |  */
   21 | export function primeLyricsFromAlbumBundle(
   22 |   bundle: AlbumLyricsBundle | null | undefined,
   23 | ) {
   24 |   if (!bundle) return;
   25 | 
   26 |   const snap = lyricsSurface.getSnapshot();
   27 | 
   28 |   const cuesIn = bundle.cuesByRecordingId ?? {};
   29 |   const offIn = bundle.offsetByRecordingId ?? {};
   30 | 
   31 |   // Build merged maps but only add keys that are currently unknown.
   32 |   let cuesOut = snap.cuesByRecordingId;
   33 |   let offOut = snap.offsetByRecordingId;
   34 | 
   35 |   for (const [recordingIdRaw, cuesRaw] of Object.entries(cuesIn)) {
   36 |     const id = (recordingIdRaw ?? "").trim();
   37 |     if (!id) continue;
   38 | 
   39 |     if (hasOwn(cuesOut, id)) continue; // don't overwrite existing (including [] known-no-lyrics)
   40 | 
   41 |     const cues = Array.isArray(cuesRaw) ? cuesRaw : [];
   42 |     cuesOut = { ...cuesOut, [id]: cues };
   43 |   }
   44 | 
   45 |   for (const [recordingIdRaw, offRaw] of Object.entries(offIn)) {
   46 |     const id = (recordingIdRaw ?? "").trim();
   47 |     if (!id) continue;
   48 | 
   49 |     if (hasOwn(offOut, id)) continue; // don't overwrite existing
   50 | 
   51 |     const n =
   52 |       typeof offRaw === "number" && Number.isFinite(offRaw)
   53 |         ? Math.floor(offRaw)
   54 |         : 0;
   55 | 
   56 |     offOut = { ...offOut, [id]: n };
   57 |   }
   58 | 
   59 |   // Only write if something changed (cheap reference check).
   60 |   if (cuesOut === snap.cuesByRecordingId && offOut === snap.offsetByRecordingId) return;
   61 | 
   62 |   lyricsSurface.setMaps({
   63 |     cuesByrecordingId: cuesOut,
   64 |     offsetByrecordingId: offOut,
   65 |     globalOffsetMs: snap.globalOffsetMs,
   66 |   });
   67 | }
   68 | 
   69 | /**
   70 |  * Ensure lyrics (cues + offset) exist in lyricsSurface for recordingId.
   71 |  * - Dedupes concurrent calls (module-level inFlight).
   72 |  * - Caches "no lyrics" as [] to avoid refetch loops.
   73 |  * - Safe to call repeatedly from any surface.
   74 |  */
   75 | export async function ensureLyricsForTrack(
   76 |   recordingId: string,
   77 |   opts?: { signal?: AbortSignal },
   78 | ): Promise<void> {
   79 |   const id = (recordingId ?? "").trim();
   80 |   if (!id) return;
   81 | 
   82 |   const snap = lyricsSurface.getSnapshot();
   83 |   const existing = snap.cuesByRecordingId[id];
   84 | 
   85 |   const knownKey = hasOwn(snap.cuesByRecordingId, id);
   86 |   const hasCues = Array.isArray(existing) && existing.length > 0;
   87 |   const knownNoLyrics =
   88 |     knownKey && Array.isArray(existing) && existing.length === 0;
   89 | 
   90 |   if (hasCues || knownNoLyrics) return;
   91 | 
   92 |   // Already fetching?
   93 |   if (inFlight.has(id)) return;
   94 | 
   95 |   const ac = new AbortController();
   96 |   inFlight.set(id, ac);
   97 | 
   98 |   // Caller abort should NOT abort the shared in-flight request.
   99 |   // It only means "this caller no longer cares". The module-scope fetch may be
  100 |   // servicing other callers (PlayerState, StageInline, etc).
  101 |   const outerSignal = opts?.signal;
  102 |   if (outerSignal?.aborted) {
  103 |     inFlight.delete(id);
  104 |     return;
  105 |   }
  106 | 
  107 |   try {
  108 |     const r = await fetchLyricsByrecordingId(id, ac.signal);
  109 |     if (!r) return;
  110 |     if (r.recordingId !== id) return;
  111 | 
  112 |     const prev = lyricsSurface.getSnapshot();
  113 | 
  114 |     const nextCuesByrecordingId =
  115 |       Array.isArray(r.cues) && r.cues.length
  116 |         ? { ...prev.cuesByRecordingId, [id]: r.cues }
  117 |         : hasOwn(prev.cuesByRecordingId, id)
  118 |           ? prev.cuesByRecordingId
  119 |           : { ...prev.cuesByRecordingId, [id]: [] };
  120 | 
  121 |     const nextOffsetByrecordingId =
  122 |       typeof r.offsetMs === "number" && Number.isFinite(r.offsetMs)
  123 |         ? { ...prev.offsetByRecordingId, [id]: r.offsetMs }
  124 |         : prev.offsetByRecordingId;
  125 | 
  126 |     lyricsSurface.setMaps({
  127 |       cuesByrecordingId: nextCuesByrecordingId,
  128 |       offsetByrecordingId: nextOffsetByrecordingId,
  129 |       // preserve whatever globalOffsetMs currently is (lyricsSurface handles defaulting)
  130 |       globalOffsetMs: prev.globalOffsetMs,
  131 |     });
  132 |   } finally {
  133 |     inFlight.delete(id);
  134 |   }
  135 | }

===== COMPLETE FILE: app/home/player/lyrics/fetchLyricsByRecordingId.ts =====
    1 | //web/app/home/player/lyrics/fetchLyricsByrecordingId.ts
    2 | "use client";
    3 | 
    4 | import { parseTrackLyricsApiOk } from "@/lib/types";
    5 | import type { LyricCue } from "@/lib/types";
    6 | 
    7 | export async function fetchLyricsByrecordingId(
    8 |   recordingId: string,
    9 |   signal?: AbortSignal,
   10 | ): Promise<{ recordingId: string; cues: LyricCue[]; offsetMs: number } | null> {
   11 |   const res = await fetch(
   12 |     `/api/lyrics/by-track?recordingId=${encodeURIComponent(recordingId)}`,
   13 |     { signal, cache: "no-store" },
   14 |   );
   15 |   if (!res.ok) return null;
   16 | 
   17 |   const raw: unknown = await res.json();
   18 |   const parsed = parseTrackLyricsApiOk(raw);
   19 |   if (!parsed) return null;
   20 | 
   21 |   return {
   22 |     recordingId: parsed.recordingId,
   23 |     cues: parsed.cues,
   24 |     offsetMs: parsed.offsetMs,
   25 |   };
   26 | }

===== COMPLETE FILE: app/home/player/lyrics/lyricBreaks.ts =====
    1 | import type { LyricCue } from "@/lib/types";
    2 | 
    3 | export const PARA_BREAK = "__PARA_BREAK__" as const;
    4 | 
    5 | export function isParaBreakCue(cue: LyricCue): boolean {
    6 |   return cue.text === PARA_BREAK;
    7 | }
    8 | 
    9 | export type LyricParagraph = { key: string; cues: LyricCue[] };
   10 | 
   11 | export function toLyricParagraphs(cues: LyricCue[]): LyricParagraph[] {
   12 |   const out: LyricParagraph[] = [];
   13 |   let cur: LyricCue[] = [];
   14 |   let paraIdx = 0;
   15 | 
   16 |   for (const c of cues) {
   17 |     if (isParaBreakCue(c)) {
   18 |       if (cur.length) {
   19 |         out.push({ key: `p-${paraIdx}`, cues: cur });
   20 |         paraIdx += 1;
   21 |       }
   22 |       cur = [];
   23 |       continue;
   24 |     }
   25 |     cur.push(c);
   26 |   }
   27 | 
   28 |   if (cur.length) out.push({ key: `p-${paraIdx}`, cues: cur });
   29 |   return out;
   30 | }

===== COMPLETE FILE: app/home/player/mediaSurface.ts =====
    1 | // web/app/home/player/mediaSurface.ts
    2 | "use client";
    3 | 
    4 | export type MediaStatus = "idle" | "loading" | "playing" | "paused" | "blocked";
    5 | export type StageVariant = "inline" | "fullscreen";
    6 | 
    7 | export type MediaEvent =
    8 |   | { type: "time"; ms: number }
    9 |   | { type: "status"; status: MediaStatus }
   10 |   | { type: "track"; id: string | null }
   11 |   | { type: "stage"; variant: StageVariant | null };
   12 | 
   13 | type Listener = (e: MediaEvent) => void;
   14 | 
   15 | class MediaSurface {
   16 |   private listeners = new Set<Listener>();
   17 |   private lastTimeMs = 0;
   18 |   private lastStatus: MediaStatus = "idle";
   19 |   private lastRecordingId: string | null = null;
   20 | 
   21 |   // Stage “authority”
   22 |   private inlineCount = 0;
   23 |   private fullscreenCount = 0;
   24 |   private activeStage: StageVariant | null = null;
   25 | 
   26 |   subscribe(fn: Listener) {
   27 |     this.listeners.add(fn);
   28 |     fn({ type: "time", ms: this.lastTimeMs });
   29 |     fn({ type: "status", status: this.lastStatus });
   30 |     fn({ type: "track", id: this.lastRecordingId });
   31 |     fn({ type: "stage", variant: this.activeStage });
   32 | 
   33 |     return () => {
   34 |       this.listeners.delete(fn);
   35 |     };
   36 |   }
   37 | 
   38 |   /* ---------------- media state ---------------- */
   39 | 
   40 |   setTime(ms: number) {
   41 |     this.lastTimeMs = ms;
   42 |     for (const fn of this.listeners) fn({ type: "time", ms });
   43 |   }
   44 | 
   45 |   setStatus(status: MediaStatus) {
   46 |     this.lastStatus = status;
   47 |     for (const fn of this.listeners) fn({ type: "status", status });
   48 |   }
   49 | 
   50 |   setTrack(id: string | null) {
   51 |     this.lastRecordingId = id;
   52 |     for (const fn of this.listeners) fn({ type: "track", id });
   53 |   }
   54 | 
   55 |   getTimeMs() {
   56 |     return this.lastTimeMs;
   57 |   }
   58 | 
   59 |   getStatus() {
   60 |     return this.lastStatus;
   61 |   }
   62 | 
   63 |   getRecordingId() {
   64 |     return this.lastRecordingId;
   65 |   }
   66 | 
   67 |   /* ---------------- stage authority ---------------- */
   68 | 
   69 |   private recomputeStage() {
   70 |     const next: StageVariant | null =
   71 |       this.fullscreenCount > 0
   72 |         ? "fullscreen"
   73 |         : this.inlineCount > 0
   74 |           ? "inline"
   75 |           : null;
   76 |     if (next === this.activeStage) return;
   77 |     this.activeStage = next;
   78 |     for (const fn of this.listeners) fn({ type: "stage", variant: next });
   79 |   }
   80 | 
   81 |   registerStage(variant: StageVariant) {
   82 |     if (variant === "fullscreen") this.fullscreenCount++;
   83 |     else this.inlineCount++;
   84 |     this.recomputeStage();
   85 | 
   86 |     return () => {
   87 |       if (variant === "fullscreen")
   88 |         this.fullscreenCount = Math.max(0, this.fullscreenCount - 1);
   89 |       else this.inlineCount = Math.max(0, this.inlineCount - 1);
   90 |       this.recomputeStage();
   91 |     };
   92 |   }
   93 | 
   94 |   getStageVariant(): StageVariant | null {
   95 |     return this.activeStage;
   96 |   }
   97 | }
   98 | 
   99 | export const mediaSurface = new MediaSurface();

   14 | 
   15 | function findActiveIndex(cues: LyricCue[], tMs: number) {
   16 |   let lo = 0;
   17 |   let hi = cues.length - 1;
   18 |   let best = -1;
   19 |   while (lo <= hi) {
   20 |     const mid = (lo + hi) >> 1;
   21 |     const v = cues[mid]?.tMs ?? 0;
   22 |     if (v <= tMs) {
   23 |       best = mid;
   24 |       lo = mid + 1;
   25 |     } else {
   26 |       hi = mid - 1;
   27 |     }
   28 |   }
   29 |   return best;
   30 | }

  135 |   }, []);
  136 | 
  137 |   function openExegesis(cue: LyricCue) {
  138 |     if (!exegesisPathToken) return;
  139 | 
  140 |     const lineKey = cue.lineKey;
  141 | 
  142 |     const path = appendPersistentSecondaryQueryToHref(
  143 |       `/exegesis/${encodeURIComponent(exegesisPathToken)}` +
  144 |         `#l=${encodeURIComponent(lineKey)}`,
  145 |     );
  146 | 
  147 |     writePendingExegesisLine(lineKey);
  148 | 
  149 |     router.push(path, { scroll: false });
  150 | 
  151 |     const detail = {
  152 |       lineKey,
  153 |       rootId: "",
  154 |       commentId: "",
  155 |     };
  156 | 
  157 |     globalThis.window.dispatchEvent(
  158 |       new CustomEvent(EXEGESIS_HASH_NAV_EVENT, { detail }),
  159 |     );
  160 | 
  161 |     globalThis.window.setTimeout(() => {
  162 |       globalThis.window.dispatchEvent(
  163 |         new CustomEvent(EXEGESIS_HASH_NAV_EVENT, { detail }),
  164 |       );
  165 |     }, 0);
  166 |   }
  167 | 
  168 |   // Fade-in whenever a new lyrics set becomes available.
  169 |   const [fadeInKey, setFadeInKey] = React.useState(0);
  170 | 
  171 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  172 |     if (!cues || cues.length === 0) return;
  173 |     // bump key so CSS animation restarts on new cues
  174 |     setFadeInKey((k) => k + 1);
  175 |   }, [cues]);
  176 | 
  177 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  178 |     activeIdxRef.current = activeIdx;
  179 |   }, [activeIdx]);
  180 | 
  181 |   // Reset on cue change.
  182 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  183 |     setActiveIdx(-1);
  184 |     setHoverIdx(-1);
  185 |     setRevealIdx(-1);
  186 |     activeIdxRef.current = -1;
  187 |     userScrollUntilRef.current = 0;
  188 |     lastFocusCenterRef.current = -1;
  189 |     isAutoScrollingRef.current = false;
  190 | 
  191 |     if (autoScrollClearRef.current)
  192 |       window.clearTimeout(autoScrollClearRef.current);
  193 |     autoScrollClearRef.current = null;
  194 | 
  195 |     const sc = scrollerRef.current;
  196 |     if (sc) {
  197 |       requestAnimationFrame(() => {
  198 |         const firstLine = sc.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-lyric-idx="0"]');
  199 |         const viewport = viewportRef.current;
  200 |         if (!firstLine || !viewport) {
  201 |           sc.scrollTop = 0;
  202 |           return;
  203 |         }
  204 | 
  205 |         const targetY =
  206 |           firstLine.offsetTop +
  207 |           firstLine.offsetHeight / 2 -
  208 |           viewport.clientHeight * 0.44;
  209 | 
  210 |         sc.scrollTop = Math.max(0, Math.round(targetY));
  211 |       });
  212 |     }
  213 | 
  214 |     lineNodeRefs.current.forEach((el) => {
  215 |       el?.style.removeProperty("--af-focus");
  216 |     });
  217 |     lineNodeRefs.current.length = cues?.length ?? 0;
  218 |   }, [cues]);
  219 | 
  220 |   // RAF: compute active index from mediaSurface time
  221 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  222 |     if (!cues || cues.length === 0) return;
  223 | 
  224 |     const step = () => {
  225 |       const tMs = mediaSurface.getTimeMs() + offsetMs;
  226 |       const idx = findActiveIndex(cues, tMs);
  227 | 
  228 |       if (idx !== activeIdxRef.current) {
  229 |         activeIdxRef.current = idx;
  230 |         setActiveIdx(idx);
  231 |       }
  232 | 
  233 |       rafTimeRef.current = window.requestAnimationFrame(step);
  234 |     };
  235 | 
  236 |     rafTimeRef.current = window.requestAnimationFrame(step);
  237 |     return () => {
  238 |       if (rafTimeRef.current) window.cancelAnimationFrame(rafTimeRef.current);
  239 |       rafTimeRef.current = null;
  240 |     };
  241 |   }, [cues, offsetMs]);
  242 | 
  243 |   // Auto-follow: scroll active line into a nice reading position, unless user recently scrolled.
  244 |   React.useLayoutEffect(() => {
  245 |     if (!cues || cues.length === 0) return;
  246 |     if (activeIdx < 0) return;
  247 | 
  248 |     const now = Date.now();
  249 |     if (now < userScrollUntilRef.current) return;
  250 | 
  251 |     const sc = scrollerRef.current;
  252 |     const viewport = viewportRef.current;
  253 |     if (!sc || !viewport) return;
  254 | 
  255 |     const activeEl = sc.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
  256 |       `[data-lyric-idx="${activeIdx}"]`,
  257 |     );
  258 |     if (!activeEl) return;
  259 | 
  260 |     const vh = viewport.clientHeight;
  261 |     if (!vh || vh < 10) return;
  262 | 
  263 |     // Keep the reading line slightly above center so upcoming lines “arrive” into the hotspot.
  264 |     const targetY = activeEl.offsetTop + activeEl.offsetHeight / 2 - vh * 0.44;
  265 |     const nextTop = clamp(
  266 |       Math.round(targetY),
  267 |       0,
  268 |       Math.max(0, sc.scrollHeight - sc.clientHeight),
  269 |     );
  270 | 
  271 |     isAutoScrollingRef.current = true;
  272 |     sc.scrollTo({ top: nextTop, behavior: "smooth" });
  273 | 
  274 |     if (autoScrollClearRef.current)
  275 |       window.clearTimeout(autoScrollClearRef.current);
  276 |     autoScrollClearRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
  277 |       isAutoScrollingRef.current = false;
  278 |     }, 220);
  279 | 
  280 |     return () => {
  281 |       if (autoScrollClearRef.current)
  282 |         window.clearTimeout(autoScrollClearRef.current);
  283 |       autoScrollClearRef.current = null;
  284 |     };
  285 |   }, [cues, activeIdx]);
  286 | 
  287 |   // DOM focus compute: uses scrollTop/offsetTop (no getBoundingClientRect spam).
  288 |   const scheduleFocusCompute = React.useCallback(() => {
  289 |     if (focusRafRef.current != null) return;
  290 |     focusRafRef.current = window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
  291 |       focusRafRef.current = null;
  292 |       const sc = scrollerRef.current;
  293 |       if (!sc) return;
  294 | 
  295 |       const center = sc.scrollTop + sc.clientHeight * 0.46;
  296 |       const falloff = Math.max(80, sc.clientHeight * (isInline ? 0.32 : 0.38));
  297 | 
  298 |       if (Math.abs(center - lastFocusCenterRef.current) < 0.5) return;
  299 |       lastFocusCenterRef.current = center;
  300 | 
  301 |       for (const el of lineNodeRefs.current) {
  302 |         if (!el) continue;
  303 |         const mid = el.offsetTop + el.offsetHeight / 2;
  304 |         const raw = 1 - Math.abs(mid - center) / falloff;
  305 |         const f = clamp(raw, 0, 1);
  306 |         el.style.setProperty("--af-focus", String(f));
  307 |       }
  308 |     });
  309 |   }, [isInline]);
  310 | 
  311 |   // Recompute focus on mount + resize + active changes (auto-follow moves).
  312 |   React.useLayoutEffect(() => {
  313 |     scheduleFocusCompute();
  314 |     const onResize = () => scheduleFocusCompute();
  315 |     window.addEventListener("resize", onResize);
  316 |     return () => window.removeEventListener("resize", onResize);
  317 |   }, [scheduleFocusCompute]);
  318 | 
  319 |   React.useLayoutEffect(() => {
  320 |     scheduleFocusCompute();
  321 |   }, [activeIdx, scheduleFocusCompute]);
  322 | 
  323 |   React.useLayoutEffect(() => {
  324 |     const sc = scrollerRef.current;
  325 |     if (!sc || !cues || cues.length === 0) return;
  326 | 
  327 |     const measure = () => {
  328 |       const lyricRows = Array.from(
  329 |         sc.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-af-lyric-real="1"]'),
  330 |       );
  331 | 
  332 |       const first = lyricRows[0] ?? null;
  333 |       const last = lyricRows.at(-1) ?? null;
  334 | 
  335 |       if (!first || !last) {
  336 |         setEdgeSpacers({ topPx: 0, bottomPx: 0 });
  337 |         return;
  338 |       }
  339 | 
  340 |       const vh = sc.clientHeight;
  341 |       if (!vh || vh < 10) return;
  342 | 
  343 |       const topPx = Math.max(
  344 |         0,
  345 |         Math.round(vh * readingCenterRatio - padTop - first.offsetHeight / 2),
  346 |       );
  347 | 
  348 |       const bottomPx = Math.max(
  349 |         0,
  350 |         Math.round(
  351 |           vh * (1 - readingCenterRatio) -
  352 |             padBottomBase -
  353 |             Math.max(0, Math.floor(reservedBottomPx)) -
  354 |             last.offsetHeight / 2,
  355 |         ),
  356 |       );
  357 | 
  358 |       setEdgeSpacers((cur) =>
  359 |         cur.topPx === topPx && cur.bottomPx === bottomPx
  360 |           ? cur
  361 |           : { topPx, bottomPx },
  362 |       );
  363 |     };
  364 | 
  365 |     measure();
  366 |     window.addEventListener("resize", measure);
  367 |     return () => window.removeEventListener("resize", measure);
  368 |   }, [cues, isInline, padTop, padBottomBase, reservedBottomPx]);
  369 |   if (!cues || cues.length === 0) {
  370 |     return (
  371 |       <div
  372 |         style={{
  373 |           position: "absolute",
  374 |           inset: 0,
  375 |           display: "grid",
  376 |           placeItems: "center",
  377 |           padding: 18,
  378 |           color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.82)",
  379 |           textAlign: "center",
  380 |           pointerEvents: "none",
  381 |         }}
  382 |       >
  383 |         <div style={{ maxWidth: 520 }}>
  384 |           <div style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 650, opacity: 0.95 }}>
  385 |             PLAY A TRACK
  386 |           </div>
  387 |         </div>
  388 |       </div>
  389 |     );
  390 |   }
  391 | 
  392 |   // Typography
  393 |   const lineFontSize = isInline
  394 |     ? "clamp(11px, 1.15vw, 13px)"
  395 |     : "clamp(18px, 2.2vw, 26px)";
  396 | 
  397 |   // Mask geometry: soften the fade "knee" to avoid horizon lines on Android.
  398 |   const fadeTopPx = isInline ? 22 : 72;
  399 |   const fadeBottomPx = isInline ? 26 : 86;
  400 |   const kneePx = isInline ? 10 : 22;
  401 | 
  402 |   // Spotlight geometry: centered around the reading zone, not the full panel.
  403 |   const spotlightCenterY = 46; // %
  404 |   const spotlightW = isInline ? 78 : 74; // %
  405 |   const spotlightH = isInline ? 40 : 44; // %
  406 | 
  407 |   // Reserve footer zone (StageTransportBar) + safe-area inset.
  408 |   // We implement this in padding and ALSO in the mask so content fades out before the controls.
  409 | 
  410 |   // Horizontal geometry:
  411 |   // - sidePad: reduce padding to increase usable line width (fewer wraps)
  412 |   // - lineMax: a centered column so text stays centered even with a right-side icon
  413 |   const sidePadPx = isInline ? 10 : 18;
  414 | 
  415 |   // IMPORTANT: keep this as a plain <length> so gridTemplateColumns never fails to parse.
  416 |   const lineMaxPx = isInline ? 820 : 980;
  417 |   const lineMax = `${lineMaxPx}px`;
  418 | 
  419 |   const discourseYOffsetPx = isInline ? 1 : 0;
  420 | 
  421 |   const styleVars: React.CSSProperties & Record<`--af-${string}`, string> = {
  422 |     "--af-lyrics-side-pad": `${sidePadPx}px`,
  423 |     "--af-lyrics-line-max": lineMax,
  424 |     "--af-discourse-y": `${discourseYOffsetPx}px`,
  425 | 
  426 |     "--af-lyrics-reserved-bottom": `${Math.max(0, Math.floor(reservedBottomPx))}px`,
  427 |     "--af-lyrics-fade-top": `${fadeTopPx}px`,
  428 |     "--af-lyrics-fade-bottom": `${fadeBottomPx}px`,
  429 |     "--af-lyrics-knee": `${kneePx}px`,
  430 |   };
  431 |   const padBottom = `calc(${padBottomBase}px + var(--af-lyrics-reserved-bottom) + env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px))`;
  432 | 
  433 |   // The point where the mask should be fully transparent at the bottom (above the transport zone).
  434 |   // Everything below this is masked out.
  435 |   const bottomClip = `calc(100% - (var(--af-lyrics-reserved-bottom) + env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px)))`;
  436 | 
  437 |   // A "soft knee" mask: no sudden slope change = no visible line.
  438 |   const mask = isInline
  439 |     ? undefined
  440 |     : `linear-gradient(
  441 |         to bottom,
  442 |         rgba(255,255,255,0) 0px,
  443 |         rgba(255,255,255,0.60) calc(var(--af-lyrics-fade-top) - var(--af-lyrics-knee)),
  444 |         rgba(255,255,255,0.92) calc(var(--af-lyrics-fade-top) - 8px),
  445 |         rgba(255,255,255,1) var(--af-lyrics-fade-top),
  446 | 
  447 |         rgba(255,255,255,1) calc(${bottomClip} - var(--af-lyrics-fade-bottom)),
  448 |         rgba(255,255,255,0.92) calc(${bottomClip} - calc(var(--af-lyrics-fade-bottom) - 8px)),
  449 |         rgba(255,255,255,0.60) calc(${bottomClip} - calc(var(--af-lyrics-fade-bottom) - var(--af-lyrics-knee))),
  450 |         rgba(255,255,255,0) ${bottomClip}
  451 |       )`;
  452 | 
  453 |   return (
  454 |     <div
  455 |       key={fadeInKey}
  456 |       style={{
  457 |         position: "absolute",
  458 |         inset: 0,
  459 |         display: "grid",
  460 |         alignItems: "stretch",
  461 |         justifyItems: "stretch",
  462 |         padding: isInline ? 8 : 14,
  463 |         pointerEvents: "auto",
  464 |         ...styleVars,
  465 | 
  466 |         // fade-in when lyrics become available / change
  467 |         opacity: 0,
  468 |         animation: isInline
  469 |           ? "afLyricsFadeIn 380ms ease-out forwards"
  470 |           : "afLyricsFadeIn 520ms ease-out forwards",
  471 |       }}
  472 |     >
  473 |       <div
  474 |         ref={viewportRef}
  475 |         style={{
  476 |           position: "relative",
  477 |           width: "100%",
  478 |           height: "100%",
  479 |           overflow: "hidden",
  480 |           background: "transparent",
  481 |           borderRadius: 0,
  482 |           border: 0,
  483 |           boxShadow: "none",
  484 |         }}
  485 |       >
  486 |         {/* Center spotlight scrim (global): dark in reading zone, transparent at edges. */}
  487 |         <div
  488 |           aria-hidden="true"
  489 |           style={{
  490 |             position: "absolute",
  491 |             inset: 0,
  492 |             pointerEvents: "none",
  493 |             zIndex: 0,
  494 |             background: `radial-gradient(${spotlightW}% ${spotlightH}% at 50% ${spotlightCenterY}%, rgba(0,0,0,${
  495 |               isInline ? 0.4 : 0.52
  496 |             }) 0%, rgba(0,0,0,0.20) 35%, rgba(0,0,0,0.00) 72%)`,
  497 |             WebkitMaskImage: `radial-gradient(${spotlightW}% ${spotlightH}% at 50% ${spotlightCenterY}%, rgba(0,0,0,1) 0%, rgba(0,0,0,0) 85%)`,
  498 |             maskImage: `radial-gradient(${spotlightW}% ${spotlightH}% at 50% ${spotlightCenterY}%, rgba(0,0,0,1) 0%, rgba(0,0,0,0) 85%)`,
  499 |             opacity: 0.95,
  500 |           }}
  501 |         />
  502 | 
  503 |         <div
  504 |           ref={scrollerRef}
  505 |           className="af-lyrics-scroll"
  506 |           onScroll={() => {
  507 |             if (!isAutoScrollingRef.current)
  508 |               userScrollUntilRef.current = Date.now() + 1400;
  509 |             scheduleFocusCompute();
  510 |           }}
  511 |           style={{
  512 |             position: "absolute",
  513 |             inset: 0,
  514 |             overflowY: "auto",
  515 |             overflowX: "hidden",
  516 |             WebkitOverflowScrolling: "touch",
  517 |             padding: `${padTop}px var(--af-lyrics-side-pad) ${padBottom} var(--af-lyrics-side-pad)`,
  518 |             display: "grid",
  519 |             gap: isInline ? 5 : 9,
  520 |             zIndex: 1,
  521 | 
  522 |             // Hide scrollbars (FF/old Edge)
  523 |             scrollbarWidth: "none",
  524 |             msOverflowStyle: "none",
  525 | 
  526 |             // Apply the edge fade here (not in Stage wrappers)
  527 |             WebkitMaskImage: mask,
  528 |             maskImage: mask,
  529 |             WebkitMaskRepeat: "no-repeat",
  530 |             maskRepeat: "no-repeat",
  531 |             WebkitMaskSize: "100% 100%",
  532 |             maskSize: "100% 100%",
  533 | 
  534 |             // Encourage compositing to reduce banding/lines on Android
  535 |             transform: "translateZ(0)",
  536 |             willChange: isInline
  537 |               ? "transform"
  538 |               : "transform, -webkit-mask-image, mask-image",
  539 |           }}
  540 |         >
  541 |           <div
  542 |             aria-hidden="true"
  543 |             style={{
  544 |               height: edgeSpacers.topPx,
  545 |               minHeight: 0,
  546 |               pointerEvents: "none",
  547 |             }}
  548 |           />
  549 |           {cues.map((cue, idx) => {
  550 |             if (isParaBreakCue(cue)) {
  551 |               // Paragraph break row: spacing only, no seek, no discourse affordance.
  552 |               return (
  553 |                 <div
  554 |                   key={`br-${cue.tMs}-${idx}`}
  555 |                   ref={(el) => {
  556 |                     lineNodeRefs.current[idx] = el;
  557 |                   }}
  558 |                   aria-hidden="true"
  559 |                   data-lyric-idx={idx}
  560 |                   className="af-lyric-row"
  561 |                   data-af-inline={isInline ? "1" : "0"}
  562 |                   style={{
  563 |                     width: "100%",
  564 |                     minWidth: 0,
  565 |                     display: "block",
  566 |                     height: isInline ? 10 : 14,
  567 |                   }}
  568 |                 />
  569 |               );
  570 |             }
  571 | 
  572 |             const isActive = idx === activeIdx;
  573 | 
  574 |             const textShadow = isInline
  575 |               ? "0 1px 14px rgba(0,0,0,0.70), 0 0 24px rgba(0,0,0,0.35)"
  576 |               : "0 2px 22px rgba(0,0,0,0.78), 0 0 34px rgba(0,0,0,0.35)";
  577 | 
  578 |             const lh = isInline ? 1.25 : 1.22;
  579 |             const scrimInset = isInline ? "-6px -10px" : "-10px -16px";
  580 |             const scrimBgStage = "rgba(0,0,0,0.18)";
  581 |             const showDiscourse =
  582 |               isInline && (idx === hoverIdx || idx === revealIdx);
  583 | 
  584 |             const iconSize = 26;
  585 |             const iconGutter = isInline ? iconSize + 8 : 0; // reserve space so text never sits under the icon
  586 | 
  587 |             return (
  588 |               <div
  589 |                 key={`${cue.tMs}-${idx}`}
  590 |                 ref={(el) => {
  591 |                   lineNodeRefs.current[idx] = el;
  592 |                 }}
  593 |                 data-lyric-idx={idx}
  594 |                 className="af-lyric-row"
  595 |                 data-af-inline={isInline ? "1" : "0"}
  596 |                 data-af-has-track={exegesisPathToken ? "1" : "0"}
  597 |                 data-af-reveal={idx === revealIdx ? "1" : "0"}
  598 |                 data-af-lyric-real="1"
  599 |                 onMouseEnter={() => {
  600 |                   if (!isInline) return;
  601 |                   setHoverIdx(idx);
  602 |                 }}
  603 |                 onMouseLeave={() => {
  604 |                   if (!isInline) return;
  605 |                   setHoverIdx((cur) => (cur === idx ? -1 : cur));
  606 |                 }}
  607 |                 style={{
  608 |                   position: "relative",
  609 |                   width: "100%",
  610 |                   minWidth: 0,
  611 |                   display: "grid",
  612 |                   justifyItems: "center",
  613 |                   alignItems: "center",
  614 |                   paddingTop: isInline ? 2 : 4,
  615 |                   paddingBottom: isInline ? 2 : 4,
  616 |                   paddingRight: iconGutter,
  617 |                 }}
  618 |               >
  619 |                 {/* Discourse icon (hover on desktop, long-press reveal on touch) */}
  620 |                 <button
  621 |                   className="af-discourse-btn"

  624 |                   title="Discuss this line"

  626 |                     e.preventDefault();
  627 |                     e.stopPropagation();
  628 |                     clearPressTimer();
  629 |                     clearRevealTimer();
  630 |                     setRevealIdx(-1);
  631 |                     openExegesis(cue);

===== COMPLETE FILE: app/home/urlState.ts =====
    1 | // web/app/home/urlState.ts
    2 | "use client";
    3 | 
    4 | import * as React from "react";
    5 | 
    6 | const QS_EVENT = "af:qs-change";
    7 | 
    8 | // Secondary query policy (strict allowlist).
    9 | // Surfaces are path-based now; query is for secondary concerns only.
   10 | const ALLOW_KEYS = new Set([
   11 |   "st",
   12 |   "share",
   13 |   "autoplay",
   14 |   "gift",
   15 |   "checkout",
   16 |   "purchase",
   17 |   "purchaseAlbum",
   18 |   "post",
   19 |   "pt",
   20 | ]);
   21 | 
   22 | const ALLOW_PREFIXES = ["utm_"];
   23 | 
   24 | // Secondary query keys that should survive internal navigation.
   25 | // `st` is durable access context for share-token playback rehydration.
   26 | const PERSIST_KEYS = new Set(["st"]);
   27 | 
   28 | // Explicitly forbidden legacy/state keys (must never persist).
   29 | const FORBIDDEN_KEYS = new Set(["p", "album", "track", "t"]);
   30 | 
   31 | function isJournalPath(pathname: string): boolean {
   32 |   return pathname === "/journal" || pathname.startsWith("/journal/");
   33 | }
   34 | 
   35 | function safeGetPathname(): string {
   36 |   if (typeof window === "undefined") return "";
   37 |   return window.location.pathname || "";
   38 | }
   39 | 
   40 | function stripPathScopedKeys(params: URLSearchParams, pathname: string): void {
   41 |   if (!isJournalPath(pathname)) params.delete("post");
   42 | }
   43 | 
   44 | function safeGetSearch(): string {
   45 |   if (typeof window === "undefined") return "";
   46 |   return window.location.search || "";
   47 | }
   48 | 
   49 | function isAllowedKey(k: string): boolean {
   50 |   if (ALLOW_KEYS.has(k)) return true;
   51 |   return ALLOW_PREFIXES.some((p) => k.startsWith(p));
   52 | }
   53 | 
   54 | /** Mutates params to enforce the secondary-query policy. */
   55 | export function sanitizeSecondaryQuery(params: URLSearchParams): void {
   56 |   sanitizeSecondaryQueryForPath(params, safeGetPathname());
   57 | }
   58 | 
   59 | /** Mutates params to enforce the secondary-query policy for a destination path. */
   60 | export function sanitizeSecondaryQueryForPath(
   61 |   params: URLSearchParams,
   62 |   pathname: string,
   63 | ): void {
   64 |   // 1) kill forbidden keys always
   65 |   for (const k of Array.from(params.keys())) {
   66 |     if (FORBIDDEN_KEYS.has(k)) params.delete(k);
   67 |   }
   68 | 
   69 |   // 2) strict allowlist for everything else
   70 |   for (const k of Array.from(params.keys())) {
   71 |     if (!isAllowedKey(k)) params.delete(k);
   72 |   }
   73 | 
   74 |   // 3) normalize share -> st when present (prefer explicit st)
   75 |   const st = (params.get("st") ?? "").trim();
   76 |   const share = (params.get("share") ?? "").trim();
   77 |   if (!st && share) params.set("st", share);
   78 |   if (params.has("share")) params.delete("share");
   79 | 
   80 |   // 4) remove query keys that only make sense on specific routes
   81 |   stripPathScopedKeys(params, pathname);
   82 | 
   83 |   // 5) trim empties
   84 |   for (const [k, v] of Array.from(params.entries())) {
   85 |     if (!String(v ?? "").trim()) params.delete(k);
   86 |   }
   87 | }
   88 | 
   89 | /** Read current location query and return a policy-sanitized copy. */
   90 | export function pickSecondaryQueryFromLocation(): URLSearchParams {
   91 |   const params = new URLSearchParams(safeGetSearch().replace(/^\?/, ""));
   92 |   sanitizeSecondaryQuery(params);
   93 |   return params;
   94 | }
   95 | 
   96 | function pickPersistentSecondaryQueryFromParams(
   97 |   params: URLSearchParams,
   98 | ): URLSearchParams {
   99 |   const clean = new URLSearchParams(params);
  100 |   sanitizeSecondaryQuery(clean);
  101 | 
  102 |   for (const k of Array.from(clean.keys())) {
  103 |     if (!PERSIST_KEYS.has(k)) clean.delete(k);
  104 |   }
  105 | 
  106 |   return clean;
  107 | }
  108 | 
  109 | /** Read current location query and return only durable query state. */
  110 | export function pickPersistentSecondaryQueryFromLocation(): URLSearchParams {
  111 |   const params = new URLSearchParams(safeGetSearch().replace(/^\?/, ""));
  112 |   return pickPersistentSecondaryQueryFromParams(params);
  113 | }
  114 | 
  115 | /**
  116 |  * Appends durable secondary query state to same-origin internal hrefs.
  117 |  *
  118 |  * Preserves hash fragments, so:
  119 |  *   /exegesis/foo#l=bar
  120 |  * becomes:
  121 |  *   /exegesis/foo?st=TOKEN#l=bar
  122 |  */
  123 | export function appendPersistentSecondaryQueryToHref(href: string): string {
  124 |   if (typeof window === "undefined") return href;
  125 | 
  126 |   const persistent = pickPersistentSecondaryQueryFromLocation();
  127 |   if (Array.from(persistent.keys()).length === 0) return href;
  128 | 
  129 |   const url = new URL(href, window.location.href);
  130 |   if (url.origin !== window.location.origin) return href;
  131 | 
  132 |   const params = new URLSearchParams(url.search);
  133 |   sanitizeSecondaryQueryForPath(params, url.pathname);
  134 | 
  135 |   for (const [k, v] of persistent.entries()) {
  136 |     if (!params.has(k)) params.set(k, v);
  137 |   }
  138 | 
  139 |   sanitizeSecondaryQueryForPath(params, url.pathname);
  140 | 
  141 |   const next = params.toString();
  142 |   url.search = next ? `?${next}` : "";
  143 | 
  144 |   return `${url.pathname}${url.search}${url.hash}`;
  145 | }
  146 | 
  147 | export function useClientSearchParams(): URLSearchParams {
  148 |   const [qs, setQs] = React.useState<string>(() => safeGetSearch());
  149 | 
  150 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  151 |     const onPop = () => setQs(safeGetSearch());
  152 |     const onCustom = () => setQs(safeGetSearch());
  153 | 
  154 |     window.addEventListener("popstate", onPop);
  155 |     window.addEventListener(QS_EVENT, onCustom as EventListener);
  156 |     return () => {
  157 |       window.removeEventListener("popstate", onPop);
  158 |       window.removeEventListener(QS_EVENT, onCustom as EventListener);
  159 |     };
  160 |   }, []);
  161 | 
  162 |   return React.useMemo(() => {
  163 |     const params = new URLSearchParams((qs || "").replace(/^\?/, ""));
  164 |     sanitizeSecondaryQuery(params);
  165 |     return params;
  166 |   }, [qs]);
  167 | }
  168 | 
  169 | export function getAutoplayFlag(sp: URLSearchParams): boolean {
  170 |   const v = (sp.get("autoplay") ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
  171 |   return v === "1" || v === "true" || v === "yes";
  172 | }
  173 | 
  174 | export function usePersistentSecondaryHref(href: string): string {
  175 |   const sp = useClientSearchParams();
  176 |   const qs = React.useMemo(() => sp.toString(), [sp]);
  177 | 
  178 |   return React.useMemo(() => {
  179 |     void qs;
  180 |     return appendPersistentSecondaryQueryToHref(href);
  181 |   }, [href, qs]);
  182 | }
  183 | 
  184 | /**
  185 |  * Patch semantics:
  186 |  * - null/undefined/'' => delete
  187 |  * - otherwise => set
  188 |  *
  189 |  * IMPORTANT: This must NOT implement any portal/player surface logic.
  190 |  * Surfaces are path-based now. Query is secondary only.
  191 |  */
  192 | export function replaceQuery(patch: Record<string, string | null | undefined>) {
  193 |   if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
  194 | 
  195 |   const url = new URL(window.location.href);
  196 |   const params = new URLSearchParams(url.search);
  197 | 
  198 |   // Build a sanitized patch object WITHOUT mutating input.
  199 |   const cleanPatch: Record<string, string | null> = {};
  200 |   for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(patch)) {
  201 |     if (FORBIDDEN_KEYS.has(k)) continue;
  202 |     const sv = v == null ? "" : String(v);
  203 |     cleanPatch[k] = sv.trim() ? sv : null;
  204 |   }
  205 | 
  206 |   // Apply patch
  207 |   for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(cleanPatch)) {
  208 |     if (v == null) params.delete(k);
  209 |     else params.set(k, v);
  210 |   }
  211 | 
  212 |   // Enforce policy after patch
  213 |   sanitizeSecondaryQueryForPath(params, url.pathname);
  214 | 
  215 |   const next = params.toString();
  216 |   const cur = url.searchParams.toString();
  217 |   if (next === cur) return;
  218 | 
  219 |   url.search = next ? `?${next}` : "";
  220 |   window.history.replaceState({}, "", url.toString());
  221 |   window.dispatchEvent(new Event(QS_EVENT));
  222 | }
  223 | 
  224 | /**
  225 |  * Convenience: read a query param once, then clear it.
  226 |  */
  227 | export function useReadOnceParam(key: string): string | null {
  228 |   const sp = useClientSearchParams();
  229 |   const v = (sp.get(key) ?? "").trim() || null;
  230 |   const shownRef = React.useRef(false);
  231 | 
  232 |   React.useEffect(() => {
  233 |     if (!v) return;
  234 |     if (shownRef.current) return;
  235 |     shownRef.current = true;
  236 |     replaceQuery({ [key]: null });
  237 |   }, [v, key]);
  238 | 
  239 |   return v;
  240 | }

   94 | export type LyricCue = {
   95 |   lineKey: string;
   96 |   tMs: number;
   97 |   text: string;
   98 |   endMs?: number;
   99 | 
  100 |   /**
  101 |    * Optional enrichment: if this cue's lineKey is mapped into a canonical exegesis group.
  102 |    * Present when known; omitted otherwise.
  103 |    */
  104 |   canonicalGroupKey?: string;
  105 | };
  106 | 
  107 | export type TrackLyricsApiOk = {
  108 |   ok: true;
  109 |   recordingId: string;
  110 |   offsetMs: number;
  111 |   cues: LyricCue[];
  112 | 
  113 |   // present in API today, but optional for consumers
  114 |   version?: string;
  115 |   geniusUrl?: string | null;
  116 |   groupMap?: LyricGroupMap;
  117 | 
  118 |   // meta fields the API returns (nullable)
  119 |   trackTitle?: string | null;
  120 |   trackArtist?: string | null;
  121 |   trackCatalogueId?: string | null;
  122 |   albumTitle?: string | null;
  123 |   albumSlug?: string | null;
  124 |   albumCatalogueId?: string | null;
  125 | };
  126 | 
  127 | export function normalizeLyricCues(input: unknown): LyricCue[] {
  128 |   if (!Array.isArray(input)) return [];
  129 |   const out: LyricCue[] = [];
  130 | 
  131 |   for (const c of input) {
  132 |     if (!c || typeof c !== "object") continue;
  133 | 
  134 |     const lineKey = (c as { lineKey?: unknown }).lineKey;
  135 |     const tMs = (c as { tMs?: unknown }).tMs;
  136 |     const text = (c as { text?: unknown }).text;
  137 |     const endMs = (c as { endMs?: unknown }).endMs;
  138 |     const canonicalGroupKey = (c as { canonicalGroupKey?: unknown })
  139 |       .canonicalGroupKey;
  140 | 
  141 |     if (typeof lineKey !== "string" || lineKey.trim().length === 0) continue;
  142 |     if (typeof tMs !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(tMs) || tMs < 0) continue;
  143 |     if (typeof text !== "string" || text.trim().length === 0) continue;
  144 | 
  145 |     const cue: LyricCue = {
  146 |       lineKey: lineKey.trim(),
  147 |       tMs: Math.floor(tMs),
  148 |       text: text.trim(),
  149 |     };
  150 | 
  151 |     if (typeof endMs === "number" && Number.isFinite(endMs) && endMs >= 0) {
  152 |       cue.endMs = Math.floor(endMs);
  153 |     }
  154 | 
  155 |     // optional enrichment
  156 |     if (typeof canonicalGroupKey === "string" && canonicalGroupKey.trim()) {
  157 |       cue.canonicalGroupKey = canonicalGroupKey.trim();
  158 |     }
  159 | 
  160 |     out.push(cue);
  161 |   }
  162 | 
  163 |   out.sort((a, b) => a.tMs - b.tMs);
  164 |   return out;
  165 | }
  166 | 
  167 | export type SanityLyricCue = {
  168 |   _key?: string;
  169 |   tMs?: number;
  170 |   text?: string;
  171 |   endMs?: number;
  172 | };
  173 | 
  174 | // Adapts Sanity cue shape into canonical cue normalization.
  175 | // (Sanity uses `_key` for the line key.)
  176 | export function normalizeLyricCuesFromSanity(
  177 |   input: SanityLyricCue[] | undefined | null,
  178 | ): LyricCue[] {
  179 |   if (!Array.isArray(input)) return [];
  180 |   return normalizeLyricCues(
  181 |     input.map((c) => ({
  182 |       lineKey: c?._key,
  183 |       tMs: c?.tMs,
  184 |       text: c?.text,
  185 |       endMs: c?.endMs,
  186 |       // canonicalGroupKey not present in Sanity lyrics docs
  187 |     })),
  188 |   );
  189 | }
  190 | 
  191 | export function parseTrackLyricsApiOk(raw: unknown): TrackLyricsApiOk | null {
  192 |   if (!raw || typeof raw !== "object") return null;
  193 |   const o = raw as Record<string, unknown>;
  194 |   if (o.ok !== true) return null;
  195 | 
  196 |   if (typeof o.recordingId !== "string" || !o.recordingId.trim()) return null;
  197 |   if (typeof o.offsetMs !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(o.offsetMs))
  198 |     return null;
  199 | 
  200 |   const cues = normalizeLyricCues(o.cues);
  201 | 
  202 |   // groupMap is optional; keep it only if it looks like the right shape
  203 |   const groupMap =
  204 |     o.groupMap && typeof o.groupMap === "object" && !Array.isArray(o.groupMap)
  205 |       ? (o.groupMap as LyricGroupMap)
  206 |       : undefined;
  207 | 
  208 |   const out: TrackLyricsApiOk = {
  209 |     ok: true,
  210 |     recordingId: o.recordingId.trim(),
  211 |     offsetMs: Math.floor(o.offsetMs),
  212 |     cues,
  213 |   };
  214 | 
  215 |   // Optional fields (keep them if present, but don’t over-validate here)
  216 |   if (typeof o.version === "string" && o.version.trim())
  217 |     out.version = o.version.trim();
  218 |   if (typeof o.geniusUrl === "string" || o.geniusUrl === null)
  219 |     out.geniusUrl = o.geniusUrl as string | null;
  220 |   if (groupMap) out.groupMap = groupMap;
  221 | 
  222 |   // Nullable meta fields (type-safe, no `any`)
  223 |   const nullableMetaKeys = [
  224 |     "trackTitle",
  225 |     "trackArtist",
  226 |     "trackCatalogueId",
  227 |     "albumTitle",
  228 |     "albumSlug",
  229 |     "albumCatalogueId",
  230 |   ] as const;
  231 | 
  232 |   type NullableMetaKey = (typeof nullableMetaKeys)[number];
  233 | 
  234 |   for (const k of nullableMetaKeys) {
  235 |     const v = o[k];
  236 |     if (typeof v === "string") {
  237 |       out[k as NullableMetaKey] = v.trim() || null;
  238 |     } else if (v === null) {
  239 |       out[k as NullableMetaKey] = null;
  240 |     }
  241 |   }
  242 | 
  243 |   return out;
  244 | }
